* ==========================================
* LLPT Code Nano - ABAP (Advanced Business Application Programming)
* ==========================================

REPORT z_llpt_employee_mgmt.

TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_employee,
         emp_id   TYPE i,
         name     TYPE string,
         dept     TYPE string,
         salary   TYPE p DECIMALS 2,
       END OF ty_employee.

DATA: lt_employees TYPE TABLE OF ty_employee,
      ls_employee  TYPE ty_employee,
      lv_total_sal TYPE p DECIMALS 2 VALUE 0.

START-OF-SELECTION.
  ls_employee-emp_id = 1001.
  ls_employee-name   = 'Alice Vance'.
  ls_employee-dept   = 'Engineering'.
  ls_employee-salary = 85000.00.
  APPEND ls_employee TO lt_employees.

  ls_employee-emp_id = 1002.
  ls_employee-name   = 'Bob Ross'.
  ls_employee-dept   = 'Design'.
  ls_employee-salary = 72000.00.
  APPEND ls_employee TO lt_employees.

  LOOP AT lt_employees INTO ls_employee WHERE salary > 70000.
    ADD ls_employee-salary TO lv_total_sal.
    WRITE: / 'ID:', ls_employee-emp_id, 'Name:', ls_employee-name, 'Dept:', ls_employee-dept.
  ENDLOOP.

  WRITE: / 'Total Salary Budget:', lv_total_sal.

* ==========================================
* ABAP Modularization & Form Subroutines
* ==========================================

FORM calculate_tax USING    iv_salary TYPE p
                  CHANGING ev_tax    TYPE p.
  IF iv_salary > 80000.
    ev_tax = iv_salary * '0.25'.
  ELSE.
    ev_tax = iv_salary * '0.15'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

* ==========================================
* Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
* ==========================================
<PRE>
FORM find_highest_paid USING    it_emp TYPE TABLE OF ty_employee
                       CHANGING ev_max TYPE p.
  DATA: ls_emp TYPE ty_employee.
  ev_max = 0.
  LOOP AT it_emp INTO ls_emp.
<SUF>
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.
<MID>
    IF ls_emp-salary > ev_max.
      ev_max = ls_emp-salary.
    ENDIF.
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: ABAP

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - ABC Programming Language
# ==========================================

HOW TO RETURN max_val list:
    PUT 0 IN max
    FOR item IN list:
        IF item > max:
            PUT item IN max
    RETURN max

HOW TO PROCESS numbers:
    WRITE "Enter total numbers:"
    READ n RAW
    PUT {} IN scores
    FOR i IN {1..n}:
        WRITE "Enter value ", i, ":"
        READ val RAW
        INSERT val IN scores
    WRITE "Maximum value found: ", max_val scores

HOW TO RETURN sum_list items:
    PUT 0 IN total
    FOR x IN items:
        PUT total + x IN total
    RETURN total

# ==========================================
# Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
# ==========================================
<PRE>
HOW TO RETURN average items:
    PUT sum_list items IN total_sum
<SUF>
    RETURN total_sum / count
<MID>
    PUT size items IN count
<EOT>

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - ActionScript 3.0 (Adobe Flash / AIR)
// ==========================================

package com.llpt.ui {
    import flash.display.Sprite;
    import flash.events.Event;
    import flash.events.MouseEvent;
    import flash.text.TextField;
    import flash.text.TextFormat;

    public class CustomButton extends Sprite {
        private var label:TextField;
        private var isPressed:Boolean = false;

        public function CustomButton(buttonText:String) {
            initBackground();
            initLabel(buttonText);
            addEventListener(MouseEvent.CLICK, onClickHandler);
        }

        private function initBackground():void {
            graphics.clear();
            graphics.beginFill(isPressed ? 0x0055A5 : 0x007ACC);
            graphics.drawRoundRect(0, 0, 140, 45, 12, 12);
            graphics.endFill();
        }

        private function initLabel(textStr:String):void {
            label = new TextField();
            label.text = textStr;
            label.x = 15;
            label.y = 12;
            label.selectable = false;

            var tf:TextFormat = new TextFormat("Arial", 14, 0xFFFFFF, true);
            label.setTextFormat(tf);
            addChild(label);
        }

        private function onClickHandler(event:MouseEvent):void {
            isPressed = !isPressed;
            initBackground();
            dispatchEvent(new Event("buttonStateChanged", true));
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
// ==========================================
<PRE>
function calculateFactorial(n:uint):uint {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
<SUF>
}
<MID>
    return n * calculateFactorial(n - 1);
<EOT>

-- ==========================================
-- LLPT Code Nano - Ada 2012 / SPARK High-Integrity Systems
-- ==========================================

package LLPT.Safety_Critical is

   type Customer_ID is range 1 .. 1_000_000;
   type Account_Balance is delta 0.01 digits 12;

   type Account_Status is (Active, Pending, Suspended, Closed);

   type Bank_Account is record
      ID      : Customer_ID;
      Status  : Account_Status := Active;
      Balance : Account_Balance := 0.0;
   end record;

   -- SPARK Contract with Pre and Post conditions
   procedure Deposit (Account : in out Bank_Account;
                      Amount  : in     Account_Balance)
     with Pre  => Amount > 0.0 and then Account.Status = Active,
          Post => Account.Balance = Account.Balance'Old + Amount;

   procedure Withdraw (Account : in out Bank_Account;
                       Amount  : in     Account_Balance)
     with Pre  => Amount > 0.0 and then
                  Account.Status = Active and then
                  Account.Balance >= Amount,
          Post => Account.Balance = Account.Balance'Old - Amount;

end LLPT.Safety_Critical;

package body LLPT.Safety_Critical is

   procedure Deposit (Account : in out Bank_Account;
                      Amount  : in     Account_Balance) is
   begin
      Account.Balance := Account.Balance + Amount;
   end Deposit;

   procedure Withdraw (Account : in out Bank_Account;
                       Amount  : in     Account_Balance) is
   begin
      Account.Balance := Account.Balance - Amount;
   end Withdraw;

end LLPT.Safety_Critical;

let rec factorial n =
    if n <= 1 then 1
    else n * factorial (n - 1)

   ========================================== *)
<PRE>
let rec sum_list lst =
    match lst with
    | [] -> 0
<SUF>
<MID>
    | x :: xs -> x + sum_list xs

# Inteligência Artificial, Redes Neurais, Transformers, Machine Learning e Engenharia de Dados

# ==============================================================================
# 1. Implementação de Mecanismo de Atenção Multi-Head (Multi-Head Self-Attention)
# PyTorch / Transformer Architecture Core
# ==============================================================================

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import math
from typing import Optional

class MultiHeadAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, embed_dim: int, num_heads: int):
        super().__init__()
        assert embed_dim % num_heads == 0, "embed_dim precisa ser divisível por num_heads"
        self.embed_dim = embed_dim
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = embed_dim // num_heads

        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(embed_dim, embed_dim)
        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(embed_dim, embed_dim)
        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(embed_dim, embed_dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(embed_dim, embed_dim)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None) -> torch.Tensor:
        batch_size, seq_len, embed_dim = x.size()

        q = self.q_proj(x).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        k = self.k_proj(x).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        v = self.v_proj(x).view(batch_size, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)

        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)

        if mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(mask == 0, -1e9)

        attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        output = output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(batch_size, seq_len, embed_dim)
        return self.out_proj(output)


# ==============================================================================
# 2. Redes Neurais e Regressão Linear do Zero com NumPy
# ==============================================================================

import numpy as np

class LinearRegressionScratch:
    """Gradient Descent Linear Regression implementation using NumPy."""
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.01, epochs: int = 1000):
        self.lr = lr
        self.epochs = epochs
        self.weights = None
        self.bias = None

    def fit(self, X: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> None:
        n_samples, n_features = X.shape
        self.weights = np.zeros(n_features)
        self.bias = 0.0

        for _ in range(self.epochs):
            y_pred = np.dot(X, self.weights) + self.bias
            dw = (1 / n_samples) * np.dot(X.T, (y_pred - y))
            db = (1 / n_samples) * np.sum(y_pred - y)

            self.weights -= self.lr * dw
            self.bias -= self.lr * db

    def predict(self, X: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.dot(X, self.weights) + self.bias

# ==========================================
# Advanced PyTorch Neural Network Training Loop
# ==========================================

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim

class DeepResNetBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_features: int, out_features: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.fc1 = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features)
        self.relu = nn.ReLU()
        self.fc2 = nn.Linear(out_features, out_features)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        residual = x
        out = self.relu(self.fc1(x))
        out = self.fc2(out)
        return self.relu(out + residual)
# ==========================================
# Native PyTorch Evaluation Loop
# ==========================================

@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate_model(model, dataloader, criterion, device):
    model.eval()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for x_batch, y_batch in dataloader:
        x_batch, y_batch = x_batch.to(device), y_batch.to(device)
        outputs = model(x_batch)
        loss = criterion(outputs, y_batch)
        total_loss += loss.item()
    return total_loss / len(dataloader)

# FIM Example
<PRE>
def accuracy(y_pred: torch.Tensor, y_true: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    preds = torch.argmax(y_pred, dim=-1)
<SUF>
    return correct.item() / len(y_true)
<MID>
    correct = (preds == y_true).sum()
<EOT>

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Algol Real Idiomatic Code
// ==========================================

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Algol

fn_factorial_algol = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_algol(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_algol = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_algol = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

<PRE>
fn_calculate_sum_algol = function(a, b) {
<SUF>
}
<MID>
    return a + b;
<EOT>

# ======================================================================
### LINGUAGEM: Algorithms Data Structures
### ID: algorithms_data_structures | Seção 011/405
# ======================================================================

# Algoritmos, Estruturas de Dados, Matemática Computacional e Complexidade

# ==============================================================================
# 1. Estruturas de Dados Avançadas: Fenwick Tree (Binary Indexed Tree)
# Complexidade: Construção O(N log N) ou O(N), Consulta de Soma de Prefixo O(log N), Atualização O(log N)
# Espaço: O(N)
# ==============================================================================

class FenwickTree:
    """
    Estrutura de dados Fenwick Tree (Binary Indexed Tree) para consultas e
    atualizações eficientes de somas de prefixo em arranjos mutáveis.
    """
    def __init__(self, size: int):
        self.size = size
        self.tree = [0] * (size + 1)

    def update(self, index: int, delta: int) -> None:
        """Adiciona delta ao elemento no índice base 1."""
        while index <= self.size:
            self.tree[index] += delta
            index += index & (-index)

    def query(self, index: int) -> int:
        """Retorna a soma dos elementos no intervalo [1, index]."""
        sum_val = 0
        while index > 0:
            sum_val += self.tree[index]
            index -= index & (-index)
        return sum_val

    def range_query(self, left: int, right: int) -> int:
        """Retorna a soma no intervalo inclusivo [left, right] (base 1)."""
        return self.query(right) - self.query(left - 1)


# ==============================================================================
# 2. Algoritmos de Grafos: Menor Caminho com A* (A-Star Search)
# Complexidade de Tempo: O(E) no pior caso
# Complexidade de Espaço: O(V)
# ==============================================================================

import heapq
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional

def a_star_search(
    graph: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]],
    heuristics: Dict[str, float],
    start: str,
    goal: str
) -> Tuple[Optional[List[str]], float]:
    """
    Busca o caminho mínimo entre 'start' e 'goal' utilizando a heurística admissível.
    """
    open_set = [(heuristics[start], 0.0, start, [start])]
    visited_cost = {start: 0.0}

    while open_set:
        f_score, g_score, current, path = heapq.heappop(open_set)

        if current == goal:
            return path, g_score

        for neighbor, weight in graph.get(current, []):
            new_g = g_score + weight
            if neighbor not in visited_cost or new_g < visited_cost[neighbor]:
                visited_cost[neighbor] = new_g
                f = new_g + heuristics.get(neighbor, 0.0)
                heapq.heappush(open_set, (f, new_g, neighbor, path + [neighbor]))

    return None, float('inf')


# ==============================================================================
# 3. Matemática Computacional: Exponenciação Matricial & Fibonacci O(log N)
# Complexidade: Tempo O(log N), Espaço O(1)
# ==============================================================================

def matrix_multiply(A: List[List[int]], B: List[List[int]], mod: int) -> List[List[int]]:
    return [
        [(A[0][0]*B[0][0] + A[0][1]*B[1][0]) % mod, (A[0][0]*B[0][1] + A[0][1]*B[1][1]) % mod],
        [(A[1][0]*B[0][0] + A[1][1]*B[1][0]) % mod, (A[1][0]*B[0][1] + A[1][1]*B[1][1]) % mod]
    ]

def fibonacci_matrix_log_n(n: int, mod: int = 10**9 + 7) -> int:
    """Calcula o n-ésimo número de Fibonacci em O(log N) usando exponenciação matricial."""
    if n == 0:
        return 0
    result = [[1, 0], [0, 1]]
    base = [[1, 1], [1, 0]]
    power = n - 1

    while power > 0:
        if power % 2 == 1:
            result = matrix_multiply(result, base, mod)
        base = matrix_multiply(base, base, mod)
        power //= 2

    return result[0][0]
# ==========================================
# Native Python Breadth-First Search (BFS)
# ==========================================

from collections import deque

def bfs_shortest_path(graph: dict[str, list[str]], start: str, target: str) -> int:
    queue = deque([(start, 0)])
    visited = {start}
    while queue:
        node, dist = queue.popleft()
        if node == target:
            return dist
        for neighbor in graph.get(node, []):
            if neighbor not in visited:
                visited.add(neighbor)
                queue.append((neighbor, dist + 1))
    return -1

        if s[left] != s[right]:

        right -= 1
    return True

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Salesforce Apex & SOQL Engine
// ==========================================

public with sharing class AccountService {

    public class AccountException extends Exception {}

    public static List<Account> getActiveAccountsWithRecentOrders(Set<Id> accountIds) {
        if (accountIds == null || accountIds.isEmpty()) {
            return new List<Account>();
        }

        return [
            SELECT Id, Name, Rating, AnnualRevenue, Phone,
                   (SELECT Id, Amount, StageName, CloseDate FROM Opportunities WHERE IsClosed = false)
            FROM Account
            WHERE Id IN :accountIds AND Active__c = 'Yes'
            WITH SECURITY_ENFORCED
            ORDER BY Name ASC
        ];
    }

    public static void updateAccountRatingBulk(List<Account> accounts) {
        List<Account> accountsToUpdate = new List<Account>();

        for (Account acc : accounts) {
            if (acc.AnnualRevenue != null && acc.AnnualRevenue >= 1000000) {
                acc.Rating = 'Hot';
                accountsToUpdate.add(acc);
            } else if (acc.AnnualRevenue != null && acc.AnnualRevenue >= 100000) {
                acc.Rating = 'Warm';
                accountsToUpdate.add(acc);
            }
        }

        if (!accountsToUpdate.isEmpty()) {
            SObjectAccessDecision decision = Security.stripInaccessible(AccessType.UPDATABLE, accountsToUpdate);
            update decision.getRecords();
        }
    }
}

// Apex Trigger
trigger AccountTrigger on Account (before insert, before update) {
    if (Trigger.isBefore) {
        AccountService.updateAccountRatingBulk(Trigger.new);
    }
}

### LINGUAGEM: Assembly
### ID: assembly | Seção 020/405
# ======================================================================

;; ==========================================
;; LLPT Code Nano - Assembly (x86_64 NASM & ARM64 AAPCS)
;; ==========================================

global _start

section .data
    msg db "Hello, LLPT Code Nano Assembly Engine!", 0xA
    len equ $ - msg
    num_array dq 10, 20, 30, 40, 50
    array_size equ 5

section .bss
    sum_res resq 1

section .text

;; NASM x86_64 Syscall - Write & Exit
_start:
    ;; Calculate sum of num_array
    mov rsi, num_array
    mov rcx, array_size
    xor rax, rax        ;; accumulator = 0

sum_loop:
    add rax, [rsi]
    add rsi, 8
    loop sum_loop

    mov [sum_res], rax  ;; Store calculated sum

    ;; Write message to stdout
    mov rax, 1          ;; sys_write
    mov rdi, 1          ;; stdout
    mov rsi, msg        ;; buffer
    mov rdx, len        ;; count
    syscall

    ;; Exit program
    mov rax, 60         ;; sys_exit
    xor rdi, rdi        ;; status code 0
    syscall

;; Function: vector_add_avx2 (C calling convention: RDI = a, RSI = b, RDX = result, RCX = count)
global vector_add_avx2
vector_add_avx2:
    push rbp
    mov rbp, rsp
    xor rax, rax

.loop:
    cmp rax, rcx
    jge .done
    vmovups ymm0, [rdi + rax*4]
    vmovups ymm1, [rsi + rax*4]
    vaddps  ymm2, ymm0, ymm1
    vmovups [rdx + rax*4], ymm2
    add rax, 8
    jmp .loop

.done:
    pop rbp
    ret

;; ==========================================
;; ARM64 (AArch64) Assembly Example
;; ==========================================
/*
.global _start
.text
_start:
    // ARM64 write syscall
    mov x0, #1          // stdout
    ldr x1, =msg        // address of string
    mov x2, #38         // length
    mov x8, #64         // sys_write
    svc #0

    // ARM64 exit syscall
    mov x0, #0          // status 0
    mov x8, #93         // sys_exit
    svc #0
*/

;; ==========================================
;; FIM Examples
;; ==========================================
<PRE>global quick_square
quick_square:
    push rbp
    mov rbp, rsp
<SUF>    pop rbp
    ret
<MID>    mov rax, rdi
    imul rax, rdi

(defn factorial [n]
  (if (<= n 1)
    1
    (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))

(defn filter-positive [coll]
  (filter #(pos? %) coll))

(defn sum-list [numbers]
  (reduce + 0 numbers))

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - AWK Text Processing & Log Auditing
# ==========================================

# Usage: awk -f script.awk access.log

BEGIN {
    FS = " " # Field separator
    OFS = "\t"
    print "=========================================="
    print "HTTP Log Analysis Summary Report"
    print "=========================================="
    total_requests = 0
    total_bytes = 0
}

# Ignore empty lines or comments
/^$/ || /^#/ { next }

{
    ip = $1
    status = $9
    bytes = $10

    total_requests++
    if (bytes ~ /^[0-9]+$/) {
        total_bytes += bytes
    }

    # Count by HTTP status code
    status_count[status]++
    ip_count[ip]++
}

END {
    print "Total Requests Processed:", total_requests
    print "Total Bandwidth Transferred:", sprintf("%.2f MB", total_bytes / (1024 * 1024))
    print "\nBreakdown by Status Code:"
    for (s in status_count) {
        printf "  Status %s: %d (%.1f%%)\n", s, status_count[s], (status_count[s]/total_requests)*100
    }

    print "\nTop 5 IP Addresses:"
    # Sorting array simulation
    for (ip in ip_count) {
        if (ip_count[ip] > 10) {
            print "  ", ip, "->", ip_count[ip], "requests"
        }
    }
}

# 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

    let result = [];

# ==============================================================================

name: CI/CD Pipeline

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, develop ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install -r requirements.txt pytest ruff

      - name: Run Linter (Ruff)
        run: ruff check .

      - name: Run Pipeline Tests
        run: python -m pytest tests/


# ==============================================================================
# 2. Production Multi-Stage Dockerfile
# ==============================================================================

FROM python:3.12-slim AS builder
WORKDIR /app
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends gcc build-essential && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --user --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

FROM python:3.12-slim AS runner
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=builder /root/.local /root/.local
COPY . .
ENV PATH=/root/.local/bin:$PATH
EXPOSE 8000
CMD ["python", "models/code/llpt-code-nano/src/inference.py"]

/* C Dataset - Low-level System Programming, Data Structures & Memory Management */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

/* ==========================================
 * 1. Dynamic Array (Vector) Implementation
 * ========================================== */

typedef struct {
    int *data;
    size_t size;
    size_t capacity;
} Vector;

Vector *vector_create(size_t initial_capacity) {
    Vector *vec = (Vector *)malloc(sizeof(Vector));
    if (!vec) return NULL;

    vec->data = (int *)malloc(initial_capacity * sizeof(int));
    if (!vec->data) {
        free(vec);
        return NULL;
    }

    vec->size = 0;
    vec->capacity = initial_capacity;
    return vec;
}

bool vector_push(Vector *vec, int value) {
    if (!vec) return false;

    if (vec->size >= vec->capacity) {
        size_t new_capacity = vec->capacity * 2;
        int *new_data = (int *)realloc(vec->data, new_capacity * sizeof(int));
        if (!new_data) return false;

        vec->data = new_data;
        vec->capacity = new_capacity;
    }

    vec->data[vec->size++] = value;
    return true;
}

int vector_get(const Vector *vec, size_t index, bool *out_success) {
    if (!vec || index >= vec->size) {
        if (out_success) *out_success = false;
        return 0;
    }
    if (out_success) *out_success = true;
    return vec->data[index];
}

void vector_free(Vector *vec) {
    if (vec) {
        free(vec->data);
        free(vec);
    }
}

/* ==========================================
 * 2. Doubly Linked List
 * ========================================== */

typedef struct Node {
    int data;
    struct Node *prev;
    struct Node *next;
} Node;

typedef struct {
    Node *head;
    Node *tail;
    size_t length;
} LinkedList;

LinkedList *list_create(void) {
    LinkedList *list = (LinkedList *)malloc(sizeof(LinkedList));
    if (!list) return NULL;
    list->head = NULL;
    list->tail = NULL;
    list->length = 0;
    return list;
}

bool list_append(LinkedList *list, int data) {
    if (!list) return false;

    Node *new_node = (Node *)malloc(sizeof(Node));
    if (!new_node) return false;

    new_node->data = data;
    new_node->next = NULL;
    new_node->prev = list->tail;

    if (list->tail) {
        list->tail->next = new_node;
    } else {
        list->head = new_node;
    }

    list->tail = new_node;
    list->length++;
    return true;
}

void list_free(LinkedList *list) {
    if (!list) return;

    Node *current = list->head;
    while (current) {
        Node *next = current->next;
        free(current);
        current = next;
    }
    free(list);
}

/* ==========================================
 * 3. Hash Table with Chaining
 * ========================================== */

#define HASH_TABLE_SIZE 128

typedef struct Entry {
    char *key;
    char *value;
    struct Entry *next;
} Entry;

typedef struct {
    Entry *buckets[HASH_TABLE_SIZE];
} HashTable;

unsigned int hash(const char *key) {
    unsigned int value = 0;
    for (; *key != '\0'; key++) {
        value = value * 37 + (unsigned char)(*key);
    }
    return value % HASH_TABLE_SIZE;
}

HashTable *ht_create(void) {
    HashTable *ht = (HashTable *)calloc(1, sizeof(HashTable));
    return ht;
}

bool ht_put(HashTable *ht, const char *key, const char *value) {
    if (!ht || !key || !value) return false;

    unsigned int slot = hash(key);
    Entry *entry = ht->buckets[slot];

    while (entry) {
        if (strcmp(entry->key, key) == 0) {
            free(entry->value);
            entry->value = strdup(value);
            return true;
        }
        entry = entry->next;
    }

    Entry *new_entry = (Entry *)malloc(sizeof(Entry));
    if (!new_entry) return false;

    new_entry->key = strdup(key);
    new_entry->value = strdup(value);
    new_entry->next = ht->buckets[slot];
    ht->buckets[slot] = new_entry;
    return true;
}

const char *ht_get(HashTable *ht, const char *key) {
    if (!ht || !key) return NULL;

    unsigned int slot = hash(key);
    Entry *entry = ht->buckets[slot];

    while (entry) {
        if (strcmp(entry->key, key) == 0) {
            return entry->value;
        }
        entry = entry->next;
    }
    return NULL;
}

void ht_free(HashTable *ht) {
    if (!ht) return;

    for (int i = 0; i < HASH_TABLE_SIZE; i++) {
        Entry *entry = ht->buckets[i];
        while (entry) {
            Entry *next = entry->next;
            free(entry->key);
            free(entry->value);
            free(entry);
            entry = next;
        }
    }
    free(ht);
}

int main(void) {
    printf("--- C System Programming Examples ---\n");

    Vector *v = vector_create(4);
    for (int i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
        vector_push(v, i * 10);
    }
    printf("Vector size: %zu, capacity: %zu\n", v->size, v->capacity);
    vector_free(v);

    HashTable *ht = ht_create();
    ht_put(ht, "language", "C");
    ht_put(ht, "version", "C11");
    printf("Language: %s | Version: %s\n", ht_get(ht, "language"), ht_get(ht, "version"));
    ht_free(ht);

    return 0;
}


/* ==========================================
 * 4. Memory Pool Allocator & Circular Buffer
 * ========================================== */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <string.h>

#define BUFFER_SIZE 8

typedef struct {
    int data[BUFFER_SIZE];
    size_t head;
    size_t tail;
    size_t count;
} CircularBuffer;

void cb_init(CircularBuffer *cb) {
    cb->head = 0;
    cb->tail = 0;
    cb->count = 0;
}

bool cb_push(CircularBuffer *cb, int value) {
    if (cb->count == BUFFER_SIZE) return false; // Buffer full
    cb->data[cb->tail] = value;
    cb->tail = (cb->tail + 1) % BUFFER_SIZE;
    cb->count++;
    return true;
}

bool cb_pop(CircularBuffer *cb, int *value) {
    if (cb->count == 0) return false; // Buffer empty
    *value = cb->data[cb->head];
    cb->head = (cb->head + 1) % BUFFER_SIZE;
    cb->count--;
    return true;
}


/* ==========================================
 * 3. Instruction & System Programming Example
 * ========================================== */

/* Instruction: Implement a thread-safe Circular Buffer (FIFO) in C using mutexes and condition variables. */
/* Response: */

#include <pthread.h>

typedef struct {
    int *buffer;
    size_t head;
    size_t tail;
    size_t max_len;
    size_t count;
    pthread_mutex_t mutex;
    pthread_cond_t not_full;
    pthread_cond_t not_empty;
} CircularBuffer;

CircularBuffer *cb_init(size_t capacity) {
    CircularBuffer *cb = (CircularBuffer *)malloc(sizeof(CircularBuffer));
    if (!cb) return NULL;
    cb->buffer = (int *)malloc(capacity * sizeof(int));
    cb->head = 0;
    cb->tail = 0;
    cb->max_len = capacity;
    cb->count = 0;
    pthread_mutex_init(&cb->mutex, NULL);
    pthread_cond_init(&cb->not_full, NULL);
    pthread_cond_init(&cb->not_empty, NULL);
    return cb;
}

void cb_push(CircularBuffer *cb, int data) {
    pthread_mutex_lock(&cb->mutex);
    while (cb->count == cb->max_len) {
        pthread_cond_wait(&cb->not_full, &cb->mutex);
    }
    cb->buffer[cb->tail] = data;
    cb->tail = (cb->tail + 1) % cb->max_len;
    cb->count++;
    pthread_cond_signal(&cb->not_empty);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&cb->mutex);
}

int cb_pop(CircularBuffer *cb) {
    pthread_mutex_lock(&cb->mutex);
    while (cb->count == 0) {
        pthread_cond_wait(&cb->not_empty, &cb->mutex);
    }
    int data = cb->buffer[cb->head];
    cb->head = (cb->head + 1) % cb->max_len;
    cb->count--;
    pthread_cond_signal(&cb->not_full);
    pthread_mutex_unlock(&cb->mutex);
    return data;
}

/* ==========================================
 * 2. Memory Management & Custom Memory Arena
 * ========================================== */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>

typedef struct {
    uint8_t *buffer;
    size_t capacity;
    size_t offset;
} MemoryArena;

MemoryArena* arena_create(size_t capacity) {
    MemoryArena *arena = (MemoryArena*)malloc(sizeof(MemoryArena));
    if (!arena) return NULL;
    arena->buffer = (uint8_t*)malloc(capacity);
    if (!arena->buffer) {
        free(arena);
        return NULL;
    }
    arena->capacity = capacity;
    arena->offset = 0;
    return arena;
}

void* arena_alloc(MemoryArena *arena, size_t size) {
    size_t aligned_size = (size + 7) & ~7; // 8-byte alignment
    if (arena->offset + aligned_size > arena->capacity) {
        return NULL; // Out of memory in arena
    }
    void *ptr = &arena->buffer[arena->offset];
    arena->offset += aligned_size;
    return ptr;
}

void arena_reset(MemoryArena *arena) {
    arena->offset = 0;
}

void arena_destroy(MemoryArena *arena) {
    if (arena) {
        free(arena->buffer);
        free(arena);
    }
}

/* ==========================================
 * 3. Lock-free Atomic Lock & Threading
 * ========================================== */

#include <stdatomic.h>

typedef struct {
    atomic_flag flag;
} Spinlock;

void spinlock_init(Spinlock *lock) {
    atomic_flag_clear(&lock->flag);
}

void spinlock_lock(Spinlock *lock) {
    while (atomic_flag_test_and_set(&lock->flag)) {
        // busy wait
    }
}

void spinlock_unlock(Spinlock *lock) {
    atomic_flag_clear(&lock->flag);
}
/* ==========================================
   Native C17 Stack Data Structure
   ========================================== */

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

typedef struct {
    int *data;
    size_t capacity;
    size_t top;
} c_stack_t;

c_stack_t* c_stack_init(size_t capacity) {
    c_stack_t *s = (c_stack_t*)malloc(sizeof(c_stack_t));
    if (!s) return NULL;
    s->data = (int*)malloc(capacity * sizeof(int));
    s->capacity = capacity;
    s->top = 0;
    return s;
}

/* C FIM Example */

int c_stack_push(c_stack_t *s, int val) {
    if (s->top >= s->capacity) return 0;
<SUF>
    return 1;
}
<MID>
    s->data[s->top++] = val;
<EOT>

### LINGUAGEM: Cadence

fn_factorial_cadence = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_cadence(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_cadence = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_cadence = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

// LLPT Code Nano - Ceylon Language
shared void run() {
    print("Welcome to Ceylon Language");
    value numbers = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
    value total = numbers.fold(0)((Integer sum, Integer val) => sum + val);
    print("Total sum: ``total``");
}

shared Integer factorial(Integer n) {
    if (n <= 1) { return 1; }
    return n * factorial(n - 1);
}

# GitHub Actions Automated CI/CD Pipeline Workflow

name: Build, Test and Deploy LLPT Engine

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, develop ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main ]

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout Code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install Dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install torch transformers pytest

      - name: Run Test Suite
        run: |
          python3 models/code/llpt-code-nano/tests/test_pipeline.py

  docker-build:
    needs: test
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout Code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - name: Build Docker Image
        run: |
          docker build -t llpt-code-nano:latest .
# ==========================================
# Native GitHub Actions Artifact Upload
# ==========================================

    - name: Archive Production Artifacts
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      with:
        name: dist-files
        path: dist/

; CIL Low-Level Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

; ==========================================
; 1. Registers and Instructions
; ==========================================

section .text
global _start

_start:
    mov eax, 1        ; system call for exit
    mov ebx, 0        ; return status 0
    int 0x80          ; execute syscall

; ==========================================
; 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
; ==========================================
<PRE>
add_numbers:
    mov eax, [esp+4]
<SUF>
    ret
<MID>
    add eax, [esp+8]
<EOT>

% CLIPS Logic Programming Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

% ==========================================
% 1. Facts and Rules
% ==========================================

parent(albert, bob).
parent(bob, charlie).

ancestor(X, Y) :- parent(X, Y).
ancestor(X, Y) :- parent(X, Z), ancestor(Z, Y).

factorial(0, 1).
factorial(N, F) :-
    N > 0,
    N1 is N - 1,
    factorial(N1, F1),
    F is N * F1.

% ==========================================
% 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
% ==========================================
<PRE>
append([], L, L).
<SUF>
<MID>
append([H|T], L, [H|R]) :-
    append(T, L, R).
<EOT>

### LINGUAGEM: Clojure
### ID: clojure | Seção 056/405
# ======================================================================

;; ==========================================
;; LLPT Code Nano - Clojure Functional Programming
;; ==========================================

(ns llpt.code.nano.demo
  (:require [clojure.string :as str]
            [clojure.core.async :as async :refer [go chan <! >! alt!]]))

;; Persistent Data Structures & Sequences
(defn process-users [users]
  (->> users
       (filter :active)
       (map #(update % :name str/capitalize))
       (sort-by :age)))

;; Atom State Management
(defonce app-state (atom {:count 0 :history []}))

(defn increment-counter! [amount]
  (swap! app-state
         (fn [state]
           (let [new-count (+ (:count state) amount)]
             (-> state
                 (assoc :count new-count)
                 (update :history conj new-count))))))

;; Polymorphic Multimethods
(defmulti calculate-tax (fn [item] (:category item)))

(defmethod calculate-tax :books [item]
  0.0)

(defmethod calculate-tax :electronics [item]
  (* (:price item) 0.15))

(defmethod calculate-tax :default [item]
  (* (:price item) 0.05))

;; Core.Async Concurrent Channel Pipeline
(defn async-pipeline-demo []
  (let [c1 (chan 10)
        c2 (chan 10)]
    (go
      (>! c1 "event-1")
      (>! c1 "event-2")
      (async/close! c1))
    (go
      (loop []
        (when-let [val (<! c1)]
          (>! c2 (str/upper-case val))
          (recur)))
      (async/close! c2))
    c2))

;; Macros
(defmacro time-execution [expr]
  `(let [start# (System/nanoTime)
         result# ~expr
         elapsed# (/ (- (System/nanoTime) start#) 1e6)]
     (println (str "Execution took " elapsed# " ms"))
     result#))

;; ==========================================
;; FIM Examples
;; ==========================================
<PRE>(defn fibonacci [n]
  (loop [i n a 0 b 1]
    (if (zero? i)
      a
<SUF>)))
<MID>      (recur (dec i) b (+ a b))
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Clojure

(defn factorial [n]
  (if (<= n 1)
    1
    (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))

(defn filter-positive [coll]
  (filter #(pos? %) coll))

(defn sum-list [numbers]
  (reduce + 0 numbers))

(defn square [x]
<SUF>
)
<MID>
  (* x x)

### LINGUAGEM: Cmake Makefile
### ID: cmake_makefile | Seção 060/405
# ======================================================================

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Modern CMake & GNU Makefile Build Systems
# ==========================================

# ------------------------------------------
# CMakeLists.txt (Modern CMake 3.20+)
# ------------------------------------------
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)
project(LLPTCodeNanoEngine VERSION 1.2.0 LANGUAGES C CXX)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)

# Options
option(BUILD_SHARED_LIBS "Build shared library" OFF)
option(ENABLE_AVX2 "Enable AVX2 vector optimization" ON)

if(ENABLE_AVX2 AND (CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_ID MATCHES "GNU|Clang"))
    add_compile_options(-mavx2 -mfma -O3)
endif()

add_library(llpt_nano_core STATIC
    src/core/model.cpp
    src/core/tokenizer.cpp
    src/core/tensor.cpp
)

target_include_directories(llpt_nano_core PUBLIC
    $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>
    $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:include>
)

find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
target_link_libraries(llpt_nano_core PRIVATE Threads::Threads)

# Executable CLI
add_executable(llpt_cli src/main.cpp)
target_link_libraries(llpt_cli PRIVATE llpt_nano_core)

# ------------------------------------------
# Makefile (GNU Make)
# ------------------------------------------
# CXX = g++
# CXXFLAGS = -std=c++20 -O3 -Wall -Iinclude
# LDFLAGS = -lpthread

# SRCS = $(wildcard src/*.cpp)
# OBJS = $(SRCS:.cpp=.o)
# TARGET = bin/llpt_runner

# all: $(TARGET)

# $(TARGET): $(OBJS)
# 	@mkdir -p bin
# 	$(CXX) $(OBJS) -o $@ $(LDFLAGS)

# %.o: %.cpp
# 	$(CXX) $(CXXFLAGS) -c $< -o $@

# clean:
# 	rm -rf $(OBJS) bin/

# ==========================================
# FIM Examples
# ==========================================
<PRE>add_executable(test_runner tests/test_tensor.cpp)
<SUF>target_link_libraries(test_runner PRIVATE GTest::gtest_main)
<MID>target_link_libraries(test_runner PRIVATE llpt_nano_core)
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Cmake Makefile

### LINGUAGEM: COBOL
### ID: cobol | Seção 062/405
# ======================================================================

      *================================================================*
      * LLPT Code Nano - Modern COBOL Batch & Transaction Processing   *
      *================================================================*
       IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
       PROGRAM-ID. CUSTOMER-PROCESSOR.
       AUTHOR. LLPT-ENGINE.

       ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
       INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
       FILE-CONTROL.
           SELECT CUST-FILE ASSIGN TO "CUSTOMERS.DAT"
               ORGANIZATION IS LINE SEQUENTIAL
               FILE STATUS IS WS-CUST-STATUS.

       DATA DIVISION.
       FILE SECTION.
       FD  CUST-FILE.
       01  CUST-RECORD.
           05  CUST-ID           PIC 9(6).
           05  CUST-NAME         PIC X(30).
           05  CUST-BALANCE      PIC S9(7)V99.
           05  CUST-STATUS-CODE  PIC X(01).

       WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
       01  WS-CUST-STATUS        PIC X(02).
       01  WS-EOF-FLAG           PIC X(01) VALUE 'N'.
           88  END-OF-FILE       VALUE 'Y'.
           88  NOT-END-OF-FILE   VALUE 'N'.

       01  WS-TOTAL-COUNTERS.
           05  WS-TOTAL-RECS     PIC 9(8) COMP-3 VALUE 0.
           05  WS-TOTAL-BAL      PIC S9(9)V99 COMP-3 VALUE 0.

       PROCEDURE DIVISION.
       0000-MAIN-LOGIC.
           PERFORM 1000-INITIALIZE
           PERFORM 2000-PROCESS-RECORDS UNTIL END-OF-FILE
           PERFORM 3000-FINALIZE
           GOBACK.

       1000-INITIALIZE.
           OPEN INPUT CUST-FILE
           IF WS-CUST-STATUS NOT = "00"
               DISPLAY "ERROR OPENING FILE: " WS-CUST-STATUS
               MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
           ELSE
               PERFORM 1500-READ-RECORD
           END-IF.

       1500-READ-RECORD.
           READ CUST-FILE
               AT END
                   MOVE 'Y' TO WS-EOF-FLAG
               NOT AT END
                   ADD 1 TO WS-TOTAL-RECS
           END-READ.

       2000-PROCESS-RECORDS.
           EVALUATE CUST-STATUS-CODE
               WHEN 'A'
                   ADD CUST-BALANCE TO WS-TOTAL-BAL
                   DISPLAY "ACTIVE CUST: " CUST-ID " NAME: " CUST-NAME
               WHEN 'I'
                   DISPLAY "INACTIVE CUST: " CUST-ID
               WHEN OTHER
                   DISPLAY "SUSPENDED CUST: " CUST-ID
           END-EVALUATE
           PERFORM 1500-READ-RECORD.

       3000-FINALIZE.
           CLOSE CUST-FILE
           DISPLAY "TOTAL RECORDS READ: " WS-TOTAL-RECS
           DISPLAY "TOTAL BALANCE: " WS-TOTAL-BAL.

      *================================================================*
      * FIM Examples
      *================================================================*
<PRE>       IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
       PROGRAM-ID. CALCULATE-TAX.
       DATA DIVISION.
       WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
       01 WS-AMOUNT PIC 9(5)V99 VALUE 1000.00.
       01 WS-TAX PIC 9(5)V99.
<SUF>       PROCEDURE DIVISION.
           COMPUTE WS-TAX = WS-AMOUNT * 0.15.
           GOBACK.
<MID>       01 WS-RATE PIC V99 VALUE .15.
<EOT>

def greet(name: str) -> str:
    return f"Hello, {name} from Cobra!"

def binary_search(arr: list, target: int) -> int:
    low, high = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        elif arr[mid] < target:
            low = mid + 1
        else:
            high = mid - 1
    return -1

from typing import List

def safe_batch_access(batch: List[int], index: int, default: int = 0) -> int:
    """Retorna o item no índice se válido, caso contrário retorna o valor padrão seguro."""
    if 0 <= index < len(batch):
        return batch[index]
    return default

# Origem Python:
# async def fetch_user_data(user_id: int) -> dict:
#     await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
#     return {"id": user_id, "status": "active"}

/**
 * Equivalent JS Implementation:
 * @param {number} userId
 * @returns {Promise<{id: number, status: string}>}
 */
async function fetchUserData(userId) {
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
    return { id: userId, status: "active" };
}

# Entendimento de Requisitos, Planejamento, Geração, Conclusão, Explicação e Reparo de Código

# ==============================================================================
# 1. Reparo de Código: Bug de Recursão Infinita & Estouro de Memória Stack
# ==============================================================================

from typing import Optional

class TreeNode:
    def __init__(self, value: int, left: 'Optional[TreeNode]' = None, right: 'Optional[TreeNode]' = None):
        self.value = value
        self.left = left
        self.right = right

def sum_tree_nodes_repaired(node: Optional[TreeNode]) -> int:
    """
    Soma recursivamente todos os valores de uma árvore binária com verificação do caso-base.
    """
    if node is None:
        return 0
    return node.value + sum_tree_nodes_repaired(node.left) + sum_tree_nodes_repaired(node.right)


# ==============================================================================
# 2. Conclusão e Reparo: Busca em Largura (BFS) em Grafos
# ==============================================================================

from collections import deque

def breadth_first_search(graph: dict, start_node: str) -> list[str]:
    """Performs BFS traversal on a graph safely avoiding infinite cycles."""
    visited = set()
    queue = deque([start_node])
    visited.add(start_node)
    result = []

    while queue:
        current = queue.popleft()
        result.append(current)

        for neighbor in graph.get(current, []):
            if neighbor not in visited:
                visited.add(neighbor)
                queue.append(neighbor)

    return result
# ==========================================
# Native Python Defensive Sanitization
# ==========================================

def sanitize_string_input(input_str: str | None) -> str:
    if not input_str:
        return ""
    return input_str.strip()

fn_factorial_coldfusion = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_coldfusion(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_coldfusion = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_coldfusion = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

;; ==========================================
;; LLPT Code Nano - Common Lisp (SBCL) & CLOS Metaobject Protocol
;; ==========================================

(defpackage :llpt-nano
  (:use :cl)
  (:export #:user #:make-user #:user-id #:user-name #:user-balance
           #:process-transaction #:calculate-interest))

(in-package :llpt-nano)

;; CLOS (Common Lisp Object System) Classes
(defclass user ()
  ((id :initarg :id
       :reader user-id
       :type integer)
   (name :initarg :name
         :accessor user-name
         :type string)
   (balance :initarg :balance
            :accessor user-balance
            :initform 0.0
            :type float)))

;; Generic Functions & Methods
(defgeneric process-transaction (account amount type)
  (:documentation "Processes a deposit or withdrawal for a given CLOS user."))

(defmethod process-transaction ((acc user) (amount number) (type (eql :deposit)))
  (incf (user-balance acc) amount)
  (format t "Deposited ~A. New Balance: ~A~%" amount (user-balance acc))
  (user-balance acc))

(defmethod process-transaction ((acc user) (amount number) (type (eql :withdrawal)))
  (if (>= (user-balance acc) amount)
      (progn
        (decf (user-balance acc) amount)
        (format t "Withdrew ~A. New Balance: ~A~%" amount (user-balance acc))
        (user-balance acc))
      (error "Insufficient funds: required ~A, available ~A" amount (user-balance acc))))

;; Common Lisp Macros
(defmacro with-timing (&body body)
  `(let ((start-time (get-internal-real-time)))
     (prog1 (progn ,@body)
       (let ((elapsed (/ (- (get-internal-real-time) start-time)
                         internal-time-units-per-second)))
         (format t "Execution took ~,3F seconds~%" elapsed)))))

;; Higher-Order Functions & Iteration
(defun filter-and-map (predicate transform list)
  (loop for item in list
        when (funcall predicate item)
        collect (funcall transform item)))

// LLPT Code Nano - Component Pascal Real Idiomatic Code

program ComponentPascalDemo;

type
  TArrayOfInt = array of Integer;

function CalculateSum(const Arr: TArrayOfInt): Integer;
var
  i, Total: Integer;
begin
  Total := 0;
  for i := Low(Arr) to High(Arr) do
    Total := Total + Arr[i];
  Result := Total;
end;

procedure ProcessData;
var
  Data: TArrayOfInt;
  Res: Integer;
begin
  SetLength(Data, 3);
  Data[0] := 10;
  Data[1] := 20;
  Data[2] := 30;
  Res := CalculateSum(Data);
  Writeln('Sum result: ', Res);
end;

begin
  ProcessData;
end.

// C++ Dataset - Modern C++17/20 OOP, Smart Pointers, Templates & Concurrency

#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <mutex>
#include <thread>
#include <optional>
#include <functional>
#include <unordered_map>

// ==========================================
// 1. RAII & Smart Pointer Management
// ==========================================

class Resource {
private:
    std::string name;
public:
    explicit Resource(std::string name) : name(std::move(name)) {
        std::cout << "[Resource] Acquired: " << this->name << "\n";
    }

    ~Resource() {
        std::cout << "[Resource] Released: " << name << "\n";
    }

    void performAction() const {
        std::cout << "[Resource] Executing action on " << name << "\n";
    }
};

class ResourceManager {
private:
    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<Resource>> resources;
public:
    void addResource(std::string name) {
        resources.push_back(std::make_unique<Resource>(std::move(name)));
    }

    void executeAll() const {
        for (const auto& res : resources) {
            res->performAction();
        }
    }
};

// ==========================================
// 2. Thread-Safe Concurrent Queue
// ==========================================

template <typename T>
class ThreadSafeQueue {
private:
    mutable std::mutex mtx;
    std::vector<T> data;
public:
    ThreadSafeQueue() = default;

    void push(T value) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mtx);
        data.push_back(std::move(value));
    }

    std::optional<T> pop() {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mtx);
        if (data.empty()) return std::nullopt;
        T val = std::move(data.back());
        data.pop_back();
        return val;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] size_t size() const {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mtx);
        return data.size();
    }
};

// ==========================================
// 3. Variadic Templates & Functional Utilities
// ==========================================

template <typename... Args>
void logMessage(Args&&... args) {
    (std::cout << ... << std::forward<Args>(args)) << std::endl;
}

template <typename Container, typename Predicate>
auto filterContainer(const Container& container, Predicate pred) {
    Container result;
    std::copy_if(container.begin(), container.end(), std::back_inserter(result), pred);
    return result;
}

// ==========================================
// 4. Modern C++ Polymorphism & Visitor
// ==========================================

class Shape {
public:
    virtual ~Shape() = default;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual double area() const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string name() const = 0;
};

class Circle : public Shape {
private:
    double radius;
public:
    explicit Circle(double r) : radius(r) {}
    [[nodiscard]] double area() const override { return 3.141592653589793 * radius * radius; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::string name() const override { return "Circle"; }
};

class Rectangle : public Shape {
private:
    double width, height;
public:
    Rectangle(double w, double h) : width(w), height(h) {}
    [[nodiscard]] double area() const override { return width * height; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::string name() const override { return "Rectangle"; }
};

int main() {
    logMessage("=== Modern C++17/20 Examples ===");

    ResourceManager mgr;
    mgr.addResource("DatabasePool");
    mgr.addResource("SocketConnection");
    mgr.executeAll();

    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Shape>> shapes = {
        std::make_shared<Circle>(5.0),
        std::make_shared<Rectangle>(4.0, 6.0)
    };

    for (const auto& shape : shapes) {
        logMessage("Shape: ", shape->name(), " | Area: ", shape->area());
    }

    ThreadSafeQueue<int> queue;
    queue.push(42);
    queue.push(100);

    if (auto val = queue.pop()) {
        logMessage("Popped value: ", *val);
    }

    return 0;
}


// ==========================================
// 4. Modern C++20 Concepts & Thread Pool
// ==========================================

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <future>
#include <queue>
#include <thread>
#include <concepts>

template<typename T>
concept Printable = requires(T a) {
    { std::cout << a } -> std::same_as<std::ostream&>;
};

template<Printable T>
void printValue(const T& val) {
    std::cout << "Value: " << val << std::endl;
}

class ThreadPool {
    std::vector<std::thread> workers;
    std::queue<std::function<void()>> tasks;
    std::mutex queue_mutex;
    std::condition_variable cv;
    bool stop = false;

public:
    ThreadPool(size_t threads) {
        for(size_t i = 0; i < threads; ++i) {
            workers.emplace_back([this] {
                while(true) {
                    std::function<void()> task;
                    {
                        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(this->queue_mutex);
                        this->cv.wait(lock, [this]{ return this->stop || !this->tasks.empty(); });
                        if(this->stop && this->tasks.empty()) return;
                        task = std::move(this->tasks.front());
                        this->tasks.pop();
                    }
                    task();
                }
            });
        }
    }

    ~ThreadPool() {
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex);
            stop = true;
        }
        cv.notify_all();
        for(std::thread &worker : workers) worker.join();
    }
};


// ==========================================
// 3. Instruction & Modern C++ Concept Example
// ==========================================

// Instruction: Write a modern C++20 thread pool implementation using std::jthread and std::condition_variable.
// Response:

#include <queue>
#include <functional>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <mutex>

class SimpleThreadPool {
private:
    std::vector<std::jthread> workers_;
    std::queue<std::function<void()>> tasks_;
    std::mutex queue_mutex_;
    std::condition_variable cv_;
    bool stop_ = false;

public:
    explicit SimpleThreadPool(size_t threads) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < threads; ++i) {
            workers_.emplace_back([this] {
                while (true) {
                    std::function<void()> task;
                    {
                        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(this->queue_mutex_);
                        this->cv_.wait(lock, [this] { return this->stop_ || !this->tasks_.empty(); });
                        if (this->stop_ && this->tasks_.empty()) return;
                        task = std::move(this->tasks_.front());
                        this->tasks_.pop();
                    }
                    task();
                }
            });
        }
    }

    void enqueue(std::function<void()> task) {
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
            tasks_.push(task);
        }
        cv_.notify_one();
    }

    ~SimpleThreadPool() {
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex_);
            stop_ = true;
        }
        cv_.notify_all();
    }
};

#include <iostream>

#include <coroutine>

template<typename T>
concept Numeric = std::is_arithmetic_v<T>;

template<Numeric T>
T add_numeric(T a, T b) {
    return a + b;
}

// Custom Smart Pointer Implementation (Simplified UniquePtr)
template<typename T>
class UniquePtr {
private:
    T* m_ptr;
public:
    explicit UniquePtr(T* ptr = nullptr) : m_ptr(ptr) {}
    ~UniquePtr() { delete m_ptr; }

    UniquePtr(const UniquePtr&) = delete;
    UniquePtr& operator=(const UniquePtr&) = delete;

    UniquePtr(UniquePtr&& other) noexcept : m_ptr(other.m_ptr) {
        other.m_ptr = nullptr;
    }

    UniquePtr& operator=(UniquePtr&& other) noexcept {
        if (this != &other) {
            delete m_ptr;
            m_ptr = other.m_ptr;
            other.m_ptr = nullptr;
        }
        return *this;
    }

    T& operator*() const { return *m_ptr; }
    T* operator->() const { return m_ptr; }
    T* get() const { return m_ptr; }
};


// Advanced C++20 Concepts, Templates, Smart Pointers, and RAII

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <memory>
#include <concepts>
#include <algorithm>

template<typename T>
concept Numeric = std::is_arithmetic_v<T>;

template<Numeric T>
class Matrix {
private:
    size_t rows_, cols_;
    std::vector<T> data_;

public:
    Matrix(size_t rows, size_t cols) : rows_(rows), cols_(cols), data_(rows * cols, 0) {}

    T& operator()(size_t r, size_t c) {
        return data_[r * cols_ + c];
    }

    const T& operator()(size_t r, size_t c) const {
        return data_[r * cols_ + c];
    }

    Matrix<T> operator+(const Matrix<T>& other) const {
        Matrix<T> result(rows_, cols_);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < data_.size(); ++i) {
            result.data_[i] = this->data_[i] + other.data_[i];
        }
        return result;
    }
};


// C++ Thread Pool Implementation using std::thread, std::mutex, std::condition_variable

#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <thread>
#include <mutex>
#include <condition_variable>
#include <functional>
#include <future>

class ThreadPool {
private:
    std::vector<std::thread> workers;
    std::queue<std::function<void()>> tasks;
    std::mutex queue_mutex;
    std::condition_variable cv;
    bool stop = false;

public:
    ThreadPool(size_t threads) {
        for(size_t i = 0; i<threads; ++i)
            workers.emplace_back([this] {
                for(;;) {
                    std::function<void()> task;
                    {
                        std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(this->queue_mutex);
                        this->cv.wait(lock, [this]{ return this->stop || !this->tasks.empty(); });
                        if(this->stop && this->tasks.empty()) return;
                        task = std::move(this->tasks.front());
                        this->tasks.pop();
                    }
                    task();
                }
            });
    }

    ~ThreadPool() {
        {
            std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(queue_mutex);
            stop = true;
        }
        cv.notify_all();
        for(std::thread &worker : workers) worker.join();
    }
};

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Crystal Language High-Performance Object Systems
# ==========================================

module LLPT::Engine
  class Customer
    include JSON::Serializable

    getter id : Int64
    property name : String
    property email : String
    property balance : Float64
    property status : String = "active"

    def initialize(@id : Int64, @name : String, @email : String, @balance : Float64 = 0.0)
      validate!
    end

    private def validate!
      raise ArgumentError.new("Email must contain @") unless @email.includes?("@")
      raise ArgumentError.new("Name cannot be blank") if @name.empty?
    end

    def credit(amount : Float64) : Float64
      raise ArgumentError.new("Amount must be positive") if amount <= 0
      @balance += amount
    end

    def debit(amount : Float64) : Float64
      raise ArgumentError.new("Insufficient balance") if amount > @balance
      @balance -= amount
    end
  end

  class Processor
    def self.process_batch(customers : Array(Customer))
      # Concurrent Fiber processing
      channel = Channel(String).new

      customers.each do |c|
        spawn do
          sleep 0.01
          channel.send("Processed: #{c.name} (#{c.balance})")
        end
      end

      customers.size.times do
        puts channel.receive
      end
    end
  end
end

# Macro Example
macro generate_getter(name, type)
  def {{name.id}} : {{type}}
    @{{name.id}}
  end
end

// C# Dataset - Modern .NET 8 / C# 12 Web API, LINQ, Async & Design Patterns

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace LlptCode.Dataset
{
    // ==========================================
    // 1. Records, Enums & Interfaces
    // ==========================================

    public enum UserStatus
    {
        Active,
        Inactive,
        PendingApproval,
        Suspended
    }

    public record UserDto(
        Guid Id,
        string Username,
        string Email,
        UserStatus Status,
        DateTime CreatedAt
    );

    public record CreateUserRequest(string Username, string Email);

    public interface IUserService
    {
        Task<UserDto> GetByIdAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct = default);
        IAsyncEnumerable<UserDto> GetAllActiveUsersAsync(CancellationToken ct = default);
        Task<UserDto> CreateUserAsync(CreateUserRequest request, CancellationToken ct = default);
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 2. Extension Methods & Custom Exceptions
    // ==========================================

    public static class StringExtensions
    {
        public static bool IsValidEmail(this string email)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email)) return false;
            return email.Contains('@') && email.Contains('.');
        }
    }

    public class UserNotFoundException : Exception
    {
        public UserNotFoundException(Guid id) : base($"User with ID {id} was not found.") { }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 3. Service Implementation with LINQ & Async
    // ==========================================

    public class UserService : IUserService
    {
        private readonly Dictionary<Guid, UserDto> _users = new();

        public UserService()
        {
            var u1 = new UserDto(Guid.NewGuid(), "alice", "alice@example.com", UserStatus.Active, DateTime.UtcNow);
            var u2 = new UserDto(Guid.NewGuid(), "bob", "bob@example.com", UserStatus.Active, DateTime.UtcNow);
            _users[u1.Id] = u1;
            _users[u2.Id] = u2;
        }

        public async Task<UserDto> GetByIdAsync(Guid id, CancellationToken ct = default)
        {
            await Task.Delay(50, ct); // Simulate IO operation
            if (_users.TryGetValue(id, out var user))
            {
                return user;
            }
            throw new UserNotFoundException(id);
        }

        public async IAsyncEnumerable<UserDto> GetAllActiveUsersAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
        {
            var activeUsers = _users.Values
                .Where(u => u.Status == UserStatus.Active)
                .OrderBy(u => u.Username);

            foreach (var user in activeUsers)
            {
                await Task.Delay(20, ct);
                yield return user;
            }
        }

        public async Task<UserDto> CreateUserAsync(CreateUserRequest request, CancellationToken ct = default)
        {
            if (!request.Email.IsValidEmail())
            {
                throw new ArgumentException("Invalid email format", nameof(request.Email));
            }

            await Task.Delay(100, ct);
            var newUser = new UserDto(
                Guid.NewGuid(),
                request.Username,
                request.Email,
                UserStatus.Active,
                DateTime.UtcNow
            );

            _users[newUser.Id] = newUser;
            return newUser;
        }

        // Pattern Matching Example
        public string EvaluateUserStatus(UserDto user) => user.Status switch
        {
            UserStatus.Active => "User is fully operational.",
            UserStatus.PendingApproval => "Awaiting administrator validation.",
            UserStatus.Inactive or UserStatus.Suspended => "Access restricted.",
            _ => "Unknown status code."
        };
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 4. Console App Entrypoint
    // ==========================================

    public class Program
    {
        public static async Task Main(string[] args)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("=== C# .NET 8 Dataset Examples ===");

            IUserService userService = new UserService();

            var created = await userService.CreateUserAsync(new CreateUserRequest("charlie", "charlie@example.com"));
            Console.WriteLine($"Created User: {created.Username} | ID: {created.Id}");

            await foreach (var user in userService.GetAllActiveUsersAsync())
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Active User Stream: {user.Username} ({user.Email})");
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

public interface IDataRepository
{
    IAsyncEnumerable<int> GetStreamDataAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default);
}

public class DataRepository : IDataRepository
{
    public async IAsyncEnumerable<int> GetStreamDataAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++)
        {
            await Task.Delay(100, cancellationToken);
            yield return i * 100;
        }
    }
}


// ==========================================
// 3. Instruction & Response Example
// ==========================================

// Instruction: Implement an ASP.NET Core Middleware pattern in C# to log request execution time.
// Response:

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using System.Diagnostics;

public class RequestTimingMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly ILogger<RequestTimingMiddleware> _logger;

    public RequestTimingMiddleware(RequestDelegate next, ILogger<RequestTimingMiddleware> logger)
    {
        _next = next;
        _logger = logger;
    }

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        try
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
        finally
        {
            sw.Stop();
            _logger.LogInformation("HTTP {Method} {Path} responded {StatusCode} in {Elapsed}ms",
                context.Request.Method, context.Request.Path, context.Response.StatusCode, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds);
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Advanced Concurrent Pipeline & Channels (.NET 8)
// ==========================================

using System.Threading.Channels;

public class DataProcessingPipeline
{
    private readonly Channel<int> _channel = Channel.CreateUnbounded<int>();

    public async Task ProduceAsync(IEnumerable<int> data)
    {
        foreach (var item in data)
        {
            await _channel.Writer.WriteAsync(item);
        }
        _channel.Writer.Complete();
    }

    public async Task ConsumeAsync(Func<int, Task> processAction)
    {
        await foreach (var item in _channel.Reader.ReadAllAsync())
        {
            await processAction(item);
        }
    }
}


// C# LINQ, Async/Await Task Parallel Library & Pattern Matching

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace LLPT.CodeNano
{
    public record UserDto(string Id, string Name, string Email, bool IsActive);

    public class UserService
    {
        private readonly List<UserDto> _users = new();

        public async Task<UserDto?> GetUserByIdAsync(string id)
        {
            await Task.Delay(10);
            return _users.FirstOrDefault(u => u.Id == id);
        }

        public IEnumerable<string> GetActiveUserEmails()
        {
            return _users
                .Where(u => u.IsActive)
                .Select(u => u.Email.ToLowerInvariant());
        }

        public string EvaluateUserRole(UserDto user) => user switch
        {
            { IsActive: false } => "Inactive User",
            { Name: "Admin" } => "System Administrator",
            _ => "Standard User"
        };
    }
}
// ==========================================
// Native C# 12 / .NET 8 Additional Architecture
// ==========================================

namespace LLPT.CodeNano.Extensions;

public static class ConcurrentWorkerExtensions
{
    public static async Task<IEnumerable<TReturn>> ProcessParallelAsync<TInput, TReturn>(
        this IEnumerable<TInput> items,
        Func<TInput, Task<TReturn>> selector,
        int maxDegreeOfParallelism = 4)
    {
        using var semaphore = new SemaphoreSlim(maxDegreeOfParallelism);
        var tasks = items.Select(async item =>
        {
            await semaphore.WaitAsync();
            try
            {
                return await selector(item);
            }
            finally
            {
                semaphore.Release();
            }
        });
        return await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
    }
}

### LINGUAGEM: Cuda Opencl
### ID: cuda_opencl | Seção 085/405
# ======================================================================

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - High Performance CUDA & OpenCL Kernels
// ==========================================

#include <cuda_runtime.h>
#include <device_launch_parameters.h>
#include <stdio.h>

// CUDA Kernel: Vector Addition with Grid-Stride Loop
__global__ void vector_add_kernel(const float* A, const float* B, float* C, int N) {
    int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    int stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x;

    for (int i = idx; i < N; i += stride) {
        C[i] = A[i] + B[i];
    }
}

// CUDA Kernel: Parallel Matrix Multiplication with Shared Memory Tiling
#define TILE_WIDTH 16

__global__ void matrix_multiply_tiled(const float* A, const float* B, float* C, int width) {
    __shared__ float s_A[TILE_WIDTH][TILE_WIDTH];
    __shared__ float s_B[TILE_WIDTH][TILE_WIDTH];

    int bx = blockIdx.x;  int by = blockIdx.y;
    int tx = threadIdx.x; int ty = threadIdx.y;

    int Row = by * TILE_WIDTH + ty;
    int Col = bx * TILE_WIDTH + tx;

    float Pvalue = 0.0f;

    for (int ph = 0; ph < width / TILE_WIDTH; ++ph) {
        s_A[ty][tx] = A[Row * width + ph * TILE_WIDTH + tx];
        s_B[ty][tx] = B[(ph * TILE_WIDTH + ty) * width + Col];
        __syncthreads();

        for (int k = 0; k < TILE_WIDTH; ++k) {
            Pvalue += s_A[ty][k] * s_B[k][tx];
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
    if (Row < width && Col < width) {
        C[Row * width + Col] = Pvalue;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// OpenCL C Kernel Examples
// ==========================================
/*
__kernel void opencl_vector_mul(__global const float* a,
                                __global const float* b,
                                __global float* result,
                                const int count) {
    int id = get_global_id(0);
    if (id < count) {
        result[id] = a[id] * b[id];
    }
}
*/

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>__global__ void reduce_sum(const float* input, float* output, int n) {
    extern __shared__ float sdata[];
    unsigned int tid = threadIdx.x;
    unsigned int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    sdata[tid] = (i < n) ? input[i] : 0.0f;
    __syncthreads();
<SUF>    if (tid == 0) output[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0];
}
<MID>    for (unsigned int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {
        if (tid < s) {
            sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + s];
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Cuda Opencl

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Cue Real Idiomatic Code
// ==========================================

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Cue

fn_factorial_cue = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_cue(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_cue = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_cue = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

<SUF>
}
<MID>
    return a + b;
<EOT>

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

SELECT username, COUNT(*) as activity_count
FROM users u
JOIN logs l ON u.id = l.user_id
WHERE l.created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
GROUP BY username
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5
ORDER BY activity_count DESC;

# Cython Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

# ==========================================
# 1. Functions and Data Structures
# ==========================================

def greet(name: str) -> str:
    return f"Hello, {name} from Cython!"

def binary_search(arr: list, target: int) -> int:
    low, high = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        elif arr[mid] < target:
            low = mid + 1
        else:
            high = mid - 1
    return -1

# ==========================================
# 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
# ==========================================
<PRE>
def factorial(n: int) -> int:
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
<SUF>
<MID>
    return n * factorial(n - 1)
<EOT>

### LINGUAGEM: D
### ID: d_lang | Seção 091/405
# ======================================================================

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - D Language Systems & Compile-Time Function Execution (CTFE)
// ==========================================

module llpt.engine;

import std.stdio;
import std.algorithm;
import std.array;
import std.exception;
import std.conv;

struct Account {
    ulong id;
    string name;
    double balance = 0.0;
    bool isActive = true;

    void deposit(double amount) pure {
        enforce!ArgumentException(amount > 0, "Deposit amount must be > 0");
        balance += amount;
    }

    void withdraw(double amount) pure {
        enforce!ArgumentException(amount <= balance, "Insufficient funds");
        balance -= amount;
    }
}

// Compile-Time CTFE Function Execution
pure double calculateCompoundInterest(double principal, double rate, int years) {
    double total = principal;
    foreach (i; 0 .. years) {
        total += total * rate;
    }
    return total;
}

// Static assert evaluated at compile time
static assert(calculateCompoundInterest(100.0, 0.05, 1) == 105.0);

// Generic Template Ranges Processing
auto processAccounts(R)(R accountsRange) if (isInputRange!R) {
    return accountsRange
        .filter!(a => a.isActive && a.balance > 100.0)
        .map!(a => tuple(a.name, a.balance))
        .array;
}

void main() {
    auto acc1 = Account(1, "D Dev", 250.0, true);
    writeln("Account balance: ", acc1.balance);
}

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>long fibonacci(int n) pure {
    if (n <= 0) return 0;
    if (n == 1) return 1;
<SUF>}
<MID>    return fibonacci(n - 1) + fibonacci(n - 2);
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: D

# ======================================================================
### LINGUAGEM: Database Orm Design
### ID: database_orm_design | Seção 094/405
# ======================================================================

# Database Schema Design, ORM Mapping & Optimization Techniques

# ==========================================
# 1. SQLAlchemy 2.0 Async ORM Mapping (Python)
# ==========================================
```python
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import String, Integer, ForeignKey, DateTime, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass

class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    email: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, server_default=func.now())

    posts: Mapped[List["Post"]] = relationship("Post", back_populates="author", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class Post(Base):
    __tablename__ = "posts"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, nullable=False)
    author_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False)

    author: Mapped[User] = relationship("User", back_populates="posts")
```

# ==========================================
# 2. Database Indexing & Query Tuning
# ==========================================
```sql
-- Composite index for fast searching on active user orders ordered by date
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_user_status_date ON orders (user_id, status, created_at DESC);

-- Partial index for high-cardinality pending transactions
CREATE INDEX idx_pending_transactions ON transactions (account_id, created_at)
WHERE status = 'PENDING';
```
-- ==========================================
-- Native SQL Composite Index Design
-- ==========================================

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_status_created
ON users(status, created_at DESC);

FROM users
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'

<MID>
ORDER BY created_at DESC
<EOT>
*/
