### ID: delphi_pascal | Seção 096/405
# ======================================================================

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Delphi RAD Studio & VCL/FMX Architecture
// ==========================================

unit LLPT.DataService;

interface

uses
  System.SysUtils, System.Classes, System.Generics.Collections, System.JSON;

type
  TAccountStatus = (asActive, asPending, asSuspended);

  TAccount = class
  private
    FId: Int64;
    FName: string;
    FBalance: Double;
    FStatus: TAccountStatus;
    procedure SetBalance(const Value: Double);
  public
    constructor Create(AId: Int64; const AName: string);
    function ToJSONObject: TJSONObject;
    property Id: Int64 read FId;
    property Name: string read FName write FName;
    property Balance: Double read FBalance write SetBalance;
    property Status: TAccountStatus read FStatus write FStatus;
  end;

  TAccountRepository = class
  private
    FList: TObjectList<TAccount>;
  public
    constructor Create;
    destructor Destroy; override;
    procedure AddAccount(AAccount: TAccount);
    function FindById(AId: Int64): TAccount;
    function GetActiveAccounts: TList<TAccount>;
  end;

implementation

{ TAccount }

constructor TAccount.Create(AId: Int64; const AName: string);
begin
  inherited Create;
  FId := AId;
  FName := AName;
  FBalance := 0.0;
  FStatus := asActive;
end;

procedure TAccount.SetBalance(const Value: Double);
begin
  if Value < 0 then
    raise EArgumentOutOfRangeException.Create('Balance cannot be negative');
  FBalance := Value;
end;

function TAccount.ToJSONObject: TJSONObject;
begin
  Result := TJSONObject.Create;
  Result.AddPair('id', TJSONNumber.Create(FId));
  Result.AddPair('name', FName);
  Result.AddPair('balance', TJSONNumber.Create(FBalance));
end;

{ TAccountRepository }

constructor TAccountRepository.Create;
begin
  inherited Create;
  FList := TObjectList<TAccount>.Create(True);
end;

destructor TAccountRepository.Destroy;
begin
  FList.Free;
  inherited Destroy;
end;

procedure TAccountRepository.AddAccount(AAccount: TAccount);
begin
  FList.Add(AAccount);
end;

function TAccountRepository.FindById(AId: Int64): TAccount;
var
  Acc: TAccount;
begin
  Result := nil;
  for Acc in FList do
  begin
    if Acc.Id = AId then
      Exit(Acc);
  end;
end;

function TAccountRepository.GetActiveAccounts: TList<TAccount>;
var
  Acc: TAccount;
begin
  Result := TList<TAccount>.Create;
  for Acc in FList do
  begin
    if Acc.Status = asActive then
      Result.Add(Acc);
  end;
end;

end.

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>function CalculateDiscount(const APrice, ARate: Double): Double;
begin
  if APrice <= 0 then Exit(0.0);
<SUF>end;
<MID>  Result := APrice * (1.0 - ARate);
<EOT>

# Linux, Redes, Infraestrutura, Deploy, Containers, Segurança e Privacidade

# ==============================================================================
# 1. Dockerfile Multi-Stage Build Otimizado para Produção Python / FastAPI
# ==============================================================================

FROM python:3.12-slim AS builder

WORKDIR /app

ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    build-essential \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --prefix=/install -r requirements.txt

FROM python:3.12-slim AS runner

WORKDIR /app

RUN useradd -m -u 1000 appuser

COPY --from=builder /install /usr/local
COPY . /app

USER appuser

EXPOSE 8000

CMD ["uvicorn", "main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]


# ==============================================================================
# 2. Prevenção de SQL Injection (OWASP Top 10) em Python/PostgreSQL
# ==============================================================================

def get_user_secure(cursor, username: str):
    """Executes safe parameterized SQL query preventing SQL injection."""
    query = "SELECT user_id, username, email, is_active FROM users WHERE username = %s LIMIT 1"
    cursor.execute(query, (username,))
    return cursor.fetchone()

# ==========================================
# Dockerfile Multi-stage Build & Linux Hardening
# ==========================================

# Stage 1: Build
FROM golang:1.22-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app
COPY go.mod go.sum ./
RUN go mod download
COPY . .
RUN CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux go build -o app .

# Stage 2: Minimal Distroless Security Execution
FROM gcr.io/distroless/static-debian12
COPY --from=builder /app/app /app
USER nonroot:nonroot
ENTRYPOINT ["/app"]

# Dockerfile Multi-stage Build & Kubernetes Deployment Config

# --- Dockerfile ---
FROM golang:1.22-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app
COPY go.mod go.sum ./
RUN go mod download
COPY . .
RUN CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux go build -o /server main.go

FROM scratch
COPY --from=builder /server /server
EXPOSE 8080
ENTRYPOINT ["/server"]

---
# --- Kubernetes Deployment & Service ---
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: llpt-service
  labels:
    app: llpt-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: llpt-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: llpt-service
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: server
        image: registry.example.com/llpt-service:v1.0.0
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8080
        resources:
          limits:

# Stage 1: Build & Dependency Caching
FROM python:3.11-slim AS builder

WORKDIR /app

ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
    build-essential \
    curl \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --user -r requirements.txt

# Stage 2: Minimal Production Image
FROM python:3.11-slim AS runner

WORKDIR /app

# Non-root security user
RUN groupadd -g 10001 appuser && \
    useradd -u 10000 -g appuser -s /bin/bash -m appuser

COPY --from=builder /root/.local /home/appuser/.local
COPY --chown=appuser:appuser . /app

ENV PATH=/home/appuser/.local/bin:$PATH \
    PORT=8080

USER appuser

EXPOSE 8080

HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
  CMD curl -f http://localhost:8080/health || exit 1

CMD ["python", "models/code/llpt-code-nano/src/inference.py", "--port", "8080"]

fn_factorial_dylan = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_dylan(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_dylan = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_dylan = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Elixir OTP & Concurrent Functional Programming
# ==========================================

defmodule LLPT.CodeNano.WorkerPool do
  @moduledoc """
  GenServer worker process pool with dynamic supervision and state management.
  """
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  # Client API
  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def process_job(job_data) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:process, job_data})
  end

  def get_stats do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :get_stats)
  end

  # Server Callbacks
  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    state = %{processed_count: 0, errors: 0}
    {:ok, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:process, job_data}, _from, state) do
    case execute_job(job_data) do
      {:ok, result} ->
        new_state = %{state | processed_count: state.processed_count + 1}
        {:reply, {:ok, result}, new_state}

      {:error, reason} ->
        new_state = %{state | errors: state.errors + 1}
        {:reply, {:error, reason}, new_state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_stats, _from, state) do
    {:reply, state, state}
  end

  defp execute_job(data) when is_map(data) do
    # Pipeline transformation
    result =
      data
      | |> Map.put_new(:timestamp, DateTime.utc_now())
      | |> Map.update!(:payload, &String.upcase/1)

    {:ok, result}
  rescue
    e -> {:error, Exception.message(e)}
  end
end

# Concurrent Async Streams & Task Processing
defmodule LLPT.CodeNano.ParallelPipeline do
  def run_parallel_tasks(items) do
    items
    |> Task.async_stream(&process_item/1, max_concurrency: System.schedulers_online(), timeout: 5000)
    |> Enum.map(fn
      {:ok, result} -> result
      {:exit, reason} -> {:error, reason}
    end)
  end

  defp process_item(item) do
    :timer.sleep(10)
    {:processed, item * 2}
  end
end

# ======================================================================
### LINGUAGEM: Erlang
### ID: erlang | Seção 109/405
# ======================================================================

%% ==========================================
%% LLPT Code Nano - Erlang OTP & Actor Concurrency
%% ==========================================

-module(llpt_worker_server).
-behaviour(gen_server).

%% API
-export([start_link/0, async_compute/1, sync_query/1, stop/0]).

%% gen_server callbacks
-export([init/1, handle_call/3, handle_cast/2, handle_info/2, terminate/2, code_change/3]).

-record(state, {
    count = 0 :: non_neg_integer(),
    active_jobs = [] :: list()
}).

%% API Functions
start_link() ->
    gen_server:start_link({local, ?MODULE}, ?MODULE, [], []).

async_compute(JobData) ->
    gen_server:cast(?MODULE__, {compute, JobData}).

sync_query(Key) ->
    gen_server:call(?MODULE__, {query, Key}).

stop() ->
    gen_server:call(?MODULE__, stop).

%% gen_server callbacks
init([]) ->
    process_flag(trap_exit, true),
    {ok, #state{count = 0, active_jobs = []}}.

handle_call({query, Key}, _From, State) ->
    Reply = {ok, Key, State#state.count},
    {reply, Reply, State};

handle_call(stop, _From, State) ->
    {stop, normal, ok, State};

handle_call(_Request, _From, State) ->
    {reply, {error, unknown_call}, State}.

handle_cast({compute, JobData}, #state{count = C, active_jobs = Jobs} = State) ->
    NewJobs = [JobData | Jobs],
    NewState = State#state{count = C + 1, active_jobs = NewJobs},
    {noreply, NewState};

handle_cast(_Msg, State) ->
    {noreply, State}.

handle_info({'EXIT', _Pid, reason_normal}, State) ->
    {noreply, State};

handle_info(_Info, State) ->
    {noreply, State}.

terminate(_Reason, _State) ->
    ok.

code_change(_OldVsn, State, _Extra) ->
    {ok, State}.

%% FIM Examples
%% ==========================================

<MID>    N * factorial(N - 1)
<EOT>

    let timeout;
    return function executedFunction(...args) {
        const later = () => {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            <SUF>

        timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);

! ==========================================
! LLPT Code Nano - Modern Fortran 2018 Numerical Computing
! ==========================================

module linear_algebra_mod
    implicit none
    private
    public :: matrix_mult, vector_norm, solve_linear_system

contains

    pure function vector_norm(v) result(norm_val)
        real(kind=8), intent(in) :: v(:)
        real(kind=8) :: norm_val
        norm_val = sqrt(sum(v**2))
    end function vector_norm

    subroutine matrix_mult(A, B, C, n, m, p)
        integer, intent(in) :: n, m, p
        real(kind=8), intent(in) :: A(n, m), B(m, p)
        real(kind=8), intent(out) :: C(n, p)
        integer :: i, j, k

        ! OpenMP parallel loop for high performance
        !$omp parallel do collapse(2) private(i, j, k)
        do j = 1, p
            do i = 1, n
                C(i, j) = 0.0_8
                do k = 1, m
                    C(i, j) = C(i, j) + A(i, k) * B(k, j)
                end do
            end do
        end do
        !$omp end parallel do
    end subroutine matrix_mult

    subroutine solve_linear_system(A, b, x, n)
        integer, intent(in) :: n
        real(kind=8), intent(inout) :: A(n, n)
        real(kind=8), intent(inout) :: b(n)
        real(kind=8), intent(out) :: x(n)
        integer :: i, j, k, max_row
        real(kind=8) :: factor, pivot_val

        ! Gaussian elimination with partial pivoting
        do k = 1, n - 1
            max_row = k
            pivot_val = abs(A(k, k))
            do i = k + 1, n
                if (abs(A(i, k)) > pivot_val) then
                    pivot_val = abs(A(i, k))
                    max_row = i
                end if
            end do

            if (max_row /= k) then
                call swap_rows(A, b, k, max_row, n)
            end if

            do i = k + 1, n
                factor = A(i, k) / A(k, k)
                A(i, k:n) = A(i, k:n) - factor * A(k, k:n)
                b(i) = b(i) - factor * b(k)
            end do
        end do

        ! Back substitution
        x(n) = b(n) / A(n, n)
        do i = n - 1, 1, -1
            x(i) = (b(i) - sum(A(i, i+1:n) * x(i+1:n))) / A(i, i)
        end do
    contains
        subroutine swap_rows(Mat, vec, r1, r2, dim_val)
            integer, intent(in) :: r1, r2, dim_val
            real(kind=8), intent(inout) :: Mat(dim_val, dim_val), vec(dim_val)
            real(kind=8) :: temp_row(dim_val), temp_v
            temp_row = Mat(r1, :)
            Mat(r1, :) = Mat(r2, :)
            Mat(r2, :) = temp_row
            temp_v = vec(r1)
            vec(r1) = vec(r2)
            vec(r2) = temp_v
        end subroutine swap_rows
    end subroutine solve_linear_system

end module linear_algebra_mod

! ==========================================
! FIM Examples
! ==========================================
<PRE>program test_fortran
    use linear_algebra_mod
    implicit none
    real(kind=8) :: vec(3)
    vec = [3.0_8, 4.0_8, 0.0_8]
<SUF>    print *, "Norm = ", vec_n
end program test_fortran
<MID>    real(kind=8) :: vec_n

(defn factorial [n]
  (if (<= n 1)
    1
    (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))

(defn filter-positive [coll]
  (filter #(pos? %) coll))

(defn sum-list [numbers]
  (reduce + 0 numbers))

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - F# Functional & Asynchronous Programming
// ==========================================

namespace LLPT.CodeNano

open System
open System.Threading.Tasks

// Discriminated Unions & Algebraic Data Types
type Result<'T, 'E> =
    | Ok of 'T
    | Error of 'E

type PaymentMethod =
    | CreditCard of cardNumber: string * expiry: string
    | PayPal of email: string
    | Crypto of walletAddress: string

type OrderState =
    | Pending
    | Processing of payment: PaymentMethod
    | Completed of transactionId: Guid
    | Failed of reason: string

// Record Type with Immutability
type Customer = {
    Id: Guid
    Name: string
    Balance: decimal
    State: OrderState
}

module CustomerOperations =

    let updateBalance (customer: Customer) (amount: decimal) : Customer =
        { customer with Balance = customer.Balance + amount }

    let processPayment (customer: Customer) (method: PaymentMethod) : Customer =
        match method with
        | CreditCard(card, _) when card.StartsWith("4") ->
            { customer with State = Completed (Guid.NewGuid()) }
        | PayPal email when email.Contains("@") ->
            { customer with State = Completed (Guid.NewGuid()) }
        | Crypto wallet when wallet.Length > 20 ->
            { customer with State = Completed (Guid.NewGuid()) }
        | _ ->
            { customer with State = Failed "Invalid payment parameters" }

    // Async & Pipeline Processing
    let fetchCustomerAsync (id: Guid) : Async<Result<Customer, string>> = async {
        do! Async.Sleep 100
        if id = Guid.Empty then
            return Error "Customer ID cannot be empty"
        else
            let customer = { Id = id; Name = "John Doe"; Balance = 500.00m; State = Pending }
            return Ok customer
    }

// Active Patterns
let (|Integer|Float|String|) (input: string) =
    match Int32.TryParse input, Double.TryParse input with
    | (true, intVal), _ -> Integer intVal
    | _, (true, floatVal) -> Float floatVal
    | _ -> String input

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================

    match n with
    | 0 -> 0
    | 1 -> 1
<SUF>    | _ -> fibonacci (n - 1) + fibonacci (n - 2)
<MID>
<EOT>

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Godot 4 GDScript Game Engine Systems
# ==========================================

class_name PlayerCharacter
extends CharacterBody2D

signal health_changed(new_health: float)
signal player_died

@export var move_speed: float = 300.0
@export var jump_velocity: float = -400.0
@export var max_health: float = 100.0

var current_health: float
var gravity: float = ProjectSettings.get_setting("physics/2d/default_gravity")

@onready var sprite: Sprite2D = $Sprite2D
@onready var animation_player: AnimationPlayer = $AnimationPlayer

func _ready() -> void:
	current_health = max_health
	emit_signal("health_changed", current_health)

func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	# Gravity application
	if not is_on_floor():
		velocity.y += gravity * delta

	# Jump handling
	if Input.is_action_just_pressed("ui_accept") and is_on_floor():
		velocity.y = jump_velocity

	# Directional movement
	var direction := Input.get_axis("ui_left", "ui_right")
	if direction != 0.0:
		velocity.x = direction * move_speed
		sprite.flip_h = (direction < 0.0)
	else:
		velocity.x = move_toward(velocity.x, 0.0, move_speed * delta * 5.0)

	move_and_slide()

func take_damage(amount: float) -> void:
	if current_health <= 0.0:
		return

	current_health = max(0.0, current_health - amount)
	emit_signal("health_changed", current_health)

	if current_health == 0.0:
		emit_signal("player_died")
		queue_free()

# ==========================================
# FIM Examples
# ==========================================

<SUF>	return xp

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Shading Languages (GLSL, HLSL, WGSL)
// ==========================================

// ------------------------------------------
// GLSL: Fragment Shader (OpenGL / WebGL)
// ------------------------------------------
#version 330 core

in vec2 TexCoords;
in vec3 Normal;
in vec3 FragPos;

out vec4 FragColor;

uniform sampler2D texture_diffuse;
uniform vec3 lightPos;
uniform vec3 viewPos;
uniform vec3 lightColor;

void main() {
    // Ambient lighting
    float ambientStrength = 0.1;
    vec3 ambient = ambientStrength * lightColor;

    // Diffuse lighting
    vec3 norm = normalize(Normal);
    vec3 lightDir = normalize(lightPos - FragPos);
    float diff = max(dot(norm, lightDir), 0.0);
    vec3 diffuse = diff * lightColor;

    // Specular lighting (Blinn-Phong)
    float specularStrength = 0.5;
    vec3 viewDir = normalize(viewPos - FragPos);
    vec3 halfwayDir = normalize(lightDir + viewDir);
    float spec = pow(max(dot(norm, halfwayDir), 0.0), 32.0);
    vec3 specular = specularStrength * spec * lightColor;

    vec4 texColor = texture(texture_diffuse, TexCoords);
    vec3 result = (ambient + diffuse + specular) * texColor.rgb;
    FragColor = vec4(result, texColor.a);
}

// ------------------------------------------
// HLSL: Compute Shader (DirectX 12)
// ------------------------------------------
/*
RWTexture2D<float4> OutputTexture : register(u0);

[numthreads(16, 16, 1)]
void CSMain(uint33 dispatchThreadID : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
    float2 uv = float2(dispatchThreadID.xy) / float2(1920.0, 1080.0);
    OutputTexture[dispatchThreadID.xy] = float4(uv.x, uv.y, 0.5, 1.0);
}
*/

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================

    vec2 e = vec2(0.001, 0.0);
<SUF>    return normalize(n);
}
<MID>    vec3 n = vec3(
        sceneSDF(p + e.xyy) - sceneSDF(p - e.xyy),
        sceneSDF(p + e.yxy) - sceneSDF(p - e.yxy),
        sceneSDF(p + e.yyx) - sceneSDF(p - e.yyx)
    );
<EOT>

# ======================================================================

// Go Dataset - Idiomatic Golang Concurrency, Microservices & Interfaces

package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// ==========================================
// 1. Data Structs & Interfaces
// ==========================================

type Role string

const (
	RoleAdmin     Role = "ADMIN"
	RoleDeveloper Role = "DEVELOPER"
	RoleGuest     Role = "GUEST"
)

type User struct {
	ID        string    `json:"id"`
	Username  string    `json:"username"`
	Email     string    `json:"email"`
	Role      Role      `json:"role"`
	IsActive  bool      `json:"is_active"`
	CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
}

type UserRepository interface {
	FindByID(ctx context.Context, id string) (*User, error)
	Save(ctx context.Context, user *User) error
	ListActive(ctx context.Context) ([]*User, error)
}

// Custom Error
var ErrUserNotFound = errors.New("user not found")

// ==========================================
// 2. In-Memory Repository Implementation
// ==========================================

type InMemoryUserRepo struct {
	mu    sync.RWMutex
	users map[string]*User
}

func NewInMemoryUserRepo() *InMemoryUserRepo {
	return &InMemoryUserRepo{
		users: make(map[string]*User),
	}
}

func (r *InMemoryUserRepo) FindByID(ctx context.Context, id string) (*User, error) {
	r.mu.RLock()
	defer r.mu.RUnlock()

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	user, exists := r.users[id]
	if !exists {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("lookup failed for ID %s: %w", id, ErrUserNotFound)
	}
	return user, nil
}

func (r *InMemoryUserRepo) Save(ctx context.Context, user *User) error {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return ctx.Err()
	default:
	}

	if user.ID == "" {
		return errors.New("user ID cannot be empty")
	}

	r.users[user.ID] = user
	return nil
}

func (r *InMemoryUserRepo) ListActive(ctx context.Context) ([]*User, error) {
	r.mu.RLock()
	defer r.mu.RUnlock()

	var activeUsers []*User
	for _, u := range r.users {
		if u.IsActive {
			activeUsers = append(activeUsers, u)
		}
	}
	return activeUsers, nil
}

// ==========================================
// 3. Concurrent Worker Pool Pattern
// ==========================================

type Task struct {
	ID    int
	Payload string
}

type Result struct {
	TaskID int
	Output string
	Err    error
}

func Worker(id int, tasks <-chan Task, results chan<- Result) {
	for t := range tasks {
		// Simulate computation or network I/O
		time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)
		results <- Result{
			TaskID: t.ID,
			Output: fmt.Sprintf("Worker %d processed task '%s'", id, t.Payload),
			Err:    nil,
		}
	}
}

func RunWorkerPool(numWorkers int, taskList []Task) []Result {
	tasks := make(chan Task, len(taskList))
	results := make(chan Result, len(taskList))

	for w := 1; w <= numWorkers; w++ {
		go Worker(w, tasks, results)
	}

	for _, t := range taskList {
		tasks <- t
	}
	close(tasks)

	var resList []Result
	for i := 0; i < len(taskList); i++ {
		resList = append(resList, <-results)
	}
	return resList
}

// ==========================================
// 4. HTTP API Handlers
// ==========================================

type UserHandler struct {
	repo UserRepository
}

func NewUserHandler(repo UserRepository) *UserHandler {
	return &UserHandler{repo: repo}
}

func (h *UserHandler) GetUserHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	id := r.URL.Query().Get("id")
	if id == "" {
		http.Error(w, "missing 'id' query parameter", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	user, err := h.repo.FindByID(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, ErrUserNotFound) {
			http.Error(w, "user not found", http.StatusNotFound)
			return
		}
		http.Error(w, "internal server error", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(user)
}

func main() {
	fmt.Println("=== Go Language Dataset Examples ===")

	repo := NewInMemoryUserRepo()
	ctx := context.Background()

	u := &User{
		ID:        "usr-101",
		Username:  "gopher",
		Email:     "gopher@golang.org",
		Role:      RoleDeveloper,
		IsActive:  true,
		CreatedAt: time.Now(),
	}

	if err := repo.Save(ctx, u); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("Failed to save user: %v", err)
	}

	found, err := repo.FindByID(ctx, "usr-101")
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("Failed to find user: %v", err)
	}
	fmt.Printf("Found User: %s (%s)\n", found.Username, found.Email)

	// Test Worker Pool
	taskList := []Task{
		{ID: 1, Payload: "Sync DB"},
		{ID: 2, Payload: "Send Emails"},
		{ID: 3, Payload: "Compress Logs"},
	}
	res := RunWorkerPool(2, taskList)
	for _, r := range res {
		fmt.Printf("[Result] Task #%d -> %s\n", r.TaskID, r.Output)
	}
}


// ==========================================
// 4. Worker Pool Pattern with Context Cancellation
// ==========================================

package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

type Job struct {
	ID    int
	Value int
}

type Result struct {
	JobID  int
	Output int
	Err    error
}

func worker(ctx context.Context, id int, jobs <-chan Job, results chan<- Result, wg *sync.WaitGroup) {
	defer wg.Done()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case job, ok := <-jobs:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			res := job.Value * 2
			results <- Result{JobID: job.ID, Output: res, Err: nil}
		}
	}
}


// ==========================================
// 3. Multi-File Go Package Structure
// ==========================================

// File: pkg/store/store.go
package store

import "errors"

var ErrNotFound = errors.New("item not found")

type Store interface {
	Get(key string) (string, error)
	Set(key, value string) error
}

// File: pkg/store/memory.go
package store

import "sync"

type MemoryStore struct {
	mu   sync.RWMutex
	data map[string]string
}

func NewMemoryStore() *MemoryStore {
	return &MemoryStore{data: make(map[string]string)}
}

func (m *MemoryStore) Get(key string) (string, error) {
	m.mu.RLock()
	defer m.mu.RUnlock()
	val, ok := m.data[key]
	if !ok {
		return "", ErrNotFound
	}
	return val, nil
}

func (m *MemoryStore) Set(key, value string) error {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	m.data[key] = value
	return nil
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Go Worker Pool & Context Cancellation
// ==========================================

package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

type Job struct {
	ID    int
	Value string
}

func Worker(ctx context.Context, id int, jobs <-chan Job, results chan<- string, wg *sync.WaitGroup) {
	defer wg.Done()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case job, ok := <-jobs:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			results <- fmt.Sprintf("Worker %d processed job %d: %s", id, job.ID, job.Value)
		}
	}
}


// Advanced Go Channels, Goroutines, Context and Interfaces

package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"time"
)

type Result struct {
	Value string
	Err   error
}

func FetchDataWithContext(ctx context.Context, url string) (string, error) {
	ch := make(chan Result, 1)

	go func() {
		// Simulate network call
		time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
		ch <- Result{Value: fmt.Sprintf("Response from %s", url), Err: nil}
	}()

	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return "", ctx.Err()
	case res := <-ch:
		return res.Value, res.Err
	}
}


// Go Microservice HTTP Server with Middleware & JSON Response

package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

type HealthResponse struct {
	Status    string
	Timestamp time.Time
}

func LoggingMiddleware(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		start := time.Now()
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
		println(r.Method, r.URL.Path, time.Since(start).String())
	})
}

func HealthCheckHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	resp := HealthResponse{Status: "UP", Timestamp: time.Now()}
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(resp)
}
// ==========================================
// Native Go 1.22 Synchronization Primitives
// ==========================================

package main

import (
	"sync"
)

type OnceCell[T any] struct {
	once  sync.Once
	value T
	err   error
}

func (c *OnceCell[T]) GetOrInit(init func() (T, error)) (T, error) {
	c.once.Do(func() {
		c.value, c.err = init()
	})
	return c.value, c.err
}

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Golo Real Idiomatic Code
// ==========================================

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Golo

fn_factorial_golo = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_golo(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_golo = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_golo = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - GraphQL Schemas, Queries & Directives
# ==========================================

"""
Directives for authentication & rate limiting
"""
directive @auth(requires: Role = USER) on OBJECT | FIELD_DEFINITION
directive @rateLimit(max: Int = 60, window: String = "1m") on FIELD_DEFINITION

enum Role {
  GUEST
  USER
  ADMIN
}

enum OrderStatus {
  PENDING
  PROCESSING
  SHIPPED
  DELIVERED
  CANCELLED
}

interface Node {
  id: ID!
  createdAt: String!
  updatedAt: String!
}

type User implements Node {
  id: ID!
  createdAt: String!
  updatedAt: String!
  username: String!
  email: String! @auth(requires: ADMIN)
  role: Role!
  orders(first: Int = 10, after: String): OrderConnection!
}

type Product implements Node {
  id: ID!
  createdAt: String!
  updatedAt: String!
  name: String!
  description: String
  price: Float!
  sku: String!
}

type Order implements Node {
  id: ID!
  createdAt: String!
  updatedAt: String!
  customer: User!
  items: [OrderItem!]!
  totalAmount: Float!
  status: OrderStatus!
}

type OrderItem {
  product: Product!
  quantity: Int!
  unitPrice: Float!
}

type OrderConnection {
  edges: [OrderEdge!]!
  pageInfo: PageInfo!
}

type OrderEdge {
  cursor: String!
  node: Order!
}

type PageInfo {
  hasNextPage: Boolean!
  endCursor: String
}

input CreateOrderInput {
  customerId: ID!
  items: [OrderItemInput!]!
}

input OrderItemInput {
  productId: ID!
  quantity: Int!
}

type Query {
  me: User @auth(requires: USER)
  user(id: ID!): User
  products(limit: Int = 20, offset: Int = 0): [Product!]! @rateLimit(max: 100)
}

type Mutation {
  createOrder(input: CreateOrderInput!): Order! @auth(requires: USER)
  updateOrderStatus(orderId: ID!, status: OrderStatus!): Order! @auth(requires: ADMIN)
}

type Subscription {
  orderUpdated(orderId: ID!): Order!
}

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Groovy, Gradle DSL & Spock Testing
// ==========================================

package com.llpt.code.nano

import groovy.transform.Canonical
import groovy.transform.ToString
import groovy.transform.CompileStatic

@CompileStatic
@Canonical
class Person {
    String firstName
    String lastName
    int age
    List<String> hobbies = []

    String getFullName() {
        return "${firstName} ${lastName}"
    }
}

class GroovyDataProcessor {

    static List<Map<String, Object>> processUsers(List<Person> people) {
        return people
            .findAll { it.age >= 18 }
            .collect { [name: it.fullName, age: it.age, isAdult: true] }
    }

    static void metaProgrammingDemo() {
        String.metaClass.toSlug = { ->
            delegate.toLowerCase().replaceAll(/[^a-z0-9]+/, '-')
        }

        def title = "LLPT Code Nano 1B Model release!"
        println title.toSlug() // Output: llpt-code-nano-1b-model-release
    }
}

// Spock Specification Example
class PersonSpec extends spock.lang.Specification {

    def "should correctly process adult persons"() {
        given: "a list of people"
        def people = [
            new Person(firstName: "Alice", lastName: "Smith", age: 25),
            new Person(firstName: "Bob", lastName: "Junior", age: 15)
        ]

        when: "processing people list"
        def results = GroovyDataProcessor.processUsers(people)

        then: "only adults are returned"
        results.size() == 1
        results[0].name == "Alice Smith"
    }
}

        APP_NAME = 'llpt-code-nano'

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Hack Language (HHVM) Strict Types
// ==========================================

<<__ConsistentConstruct>>
abstract class BaseEntity {
  public function __construct(
    protected int $id,
    protected string $createdAt,
  ) {}

  public function getId(): int {
    return $this->id;
  }
}

final class UserEntity extends BaseEntity {
  public function __construct(
    int $id,
    string $createdAt,
    private string $name,
    private string $email,
    private vec<string> $roles = vec[],
  ) {
    parent::__construct($id, $createdAt);
  }

  public function getName(): string {
    return $this->name;
  }

  public function getEmail(): string {
    return $this->email;
  }

  public function hasRole(string $targetRole): bool {
    foreach ($this->roles as $role) {
      if ($role === $targetRole) {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  public async function fetchExtraDetailsAsync(): Awaitable<dict<string, mixed>> {
    await HH\Asio\usleep(10000); // 10ms async sleep
    return dict[
      "status" => "active",
      "score" => 98.5,
    ];
  }
}

<<__EntryPoint>>
async function main_async(): Awaitable<void> {
  $user = new UserEntity(101, "2026-03-30", "Hack Dev", "hack@example.com", vec["admin", "developer"]);
  echo "User: " . $user->getName() . "\n";
  $details = await $user->fetchExtraDetailsAsync();
  echo "Status: " . (string)$details["status"] . "\n";
}

# LLPT Code Nano - Hare Real Idiomatic Code
# ==========================================

# 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Hare

fn_factorial_hare = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_hare(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_hare = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

# 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_hare = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

<SUF>
}
<MID>
    return a + b;
<EOT>

-- ==========================================
-- LLPT Code Nano - Advanced Haskell & Functional Systems
-- ==========================================

{-# LANGUAGE DeriveGeneric #-}
{-# LANGUAGE OverloadedStrings #-}
{-# LANGUAGE FlexibleContexts #-}

module Main where

import GHC.Generics
import Data.Text (Text)
import qualified Data.Text as T
import Control.Monad.State
import Control.Monad.Except

-- Algebraic Data Types & GADTs
data PaymentStatus
  = Pending
  | Processing
  | Succeeded Text
  | Failed Text
  deriving (Show, Eq, Generic)

data User = User
  { userId    :: Int
  , userName  :: Text
  , userEmail :: Text
  } deriving (Show, Eq, Generic)

-- Monad Transformer Stack
type AppState = [User]
type AppMonad a = ExceptT Text (State AppState) a

registerUser :: Text -> Text -> AppMonad User
registerUser name email = do
  users <- get
  let newId = length users + 1
  if any (\u -> userEmail u == email) users
    then throwError "User with this email already exists"
    else do
      let newUser = User newId name email
      put (newUser : users)
      return newUser

-- Higher Order Pipelines & QuickCheck
quickSort :: Ord a => [a] -> [a]
quickSort [] = []
quickSort (x:xs) =
  let smaller = quickSort [a | a <- xs, a <= x]
      larger  = quickSort [b | b <- xs, b > x]
  in smaller ++ [x] ++ larger

main :: IO ()
main = do
  let initialUsers = []
      action = do
        u1 <- registerUser "Alice" "alice@example.com"
        u2 <- registerUser "Bob" "bob@example.com"
        return [u1, u2]
      (result, finalState) = runState (runExceptT action) initialUsers
  case result of
    Right users -> putStrLn $ "Registered users: " ++ show (length users)
    Left err    -> putStrLn $ "Error: " ++ T.unpack err

-- FIM Examples
-- ==========================================

fibonacci 0 = 0
fibonacci 1 = 1
<SUF>fibonacci n = fibonacci (n - 1) + fibonacci (n - 2)
<MID>fibonacci n | n < 0 = error "Negative index"
<EOT>

terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.5.0"
  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source  = "hashicorp/aws"
      version = "~> 5.0"
    }
  }
}

provider "aws" {
  region = var.aws_region
}

variable "aws_region" {
  type        = string
  default     = "us-east-1"
  description = "AWS target deployment region"
}

variable "environment" {
  type        = string
  default     = "prod"
  description = "Deployment environment name"
}

# VPC Network
resource "aws_vpc" "main" {
  cidr_block           = "10.0.0.0/16"
  enable_dns_hostnames = true
  enable_dns_support   = true

  tags = {
    Name        = "vpc-llpt-${var.environment}"
    Environment = var.environment
  }
}

resource "aws_subnet" "public_a" {
  vpc_id                  = aws_vpc.main.id
  cidr_block              = "10.0.1.0/24"
  availability_zone       = "${var.aws_region}a"
  map_public_ip_on_launch = true

  tags = {
    Name = "subnet-public-a-${var.environment}"
  }
}

# ECS Cluster
resource "aws_ecs_cluster" "nano_cluster" {
  name = "ecs-cluster-llpt-${var.environment}"

  setting {
    name  = "containerInsights"
    value = "enabled"
  }
}

output "vpc_id" {
  value       = aws_vpc.main.id
  description = "Created VPC Identifier"
}

output "ecs_cluster_arn" {
  value       = aws_ecs_cluster.nano_cluster.arn
  description = "Amazon ECS Cluster ARN"
}

-- HiveQL Query Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

-- ==========================================
-- 1. Table Definitions and Queries
-- ==========================================

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

SELECT username, COUNT(*) as activity_count
FROM users u
JOIN logs l ON u.id = l.user_id
WHERE l.created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
GROUP BY username
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5
ORDER BY activity_count DESC;

-- ==========================================
-- 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
-- ==========================================
<PRE>
SELECT department_id, AVG(salary) as avg_sal
FROM employees
<SUF>
ORDER BY avg_sal DESC;
<MID>
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
GROUP BY department_id
<EOT>

;; ==========================================
;; LLPT Code Nano - Hy Language (Lisp Syntax for Python AST)
;; ==========================================

(import [functools [reduce]]
        [json [dumps loads]])

(defclass Customer []
  (defn --init-- [self customer-id name email [balance 0.0]]
    (setv self.customer-id customer-id)
    (setv self.name name)
    (setv self.email email)
    (setv self.balance balance))

  (defn deposit [self amount]
    (if (<= amount 0)
      (raise (ValueError "Amount must be positive"))
      (setv self.balance (+ self.balance amount)))
    self.balance)

  (defn to-dict [self]
    {"id" self.customer-id
     "name" self.name
     "email" self.email
     "balance" self.balance}))

(defn calculate-total-balance [customers]
  (reduce (fn [acc cust] (+ acc cust.balance)) customers 0.0))

(defn main []
  (setv c1 (Customer 101 "Hy Developer" "hy@example.com" 150.75))
  (c1.deposit 50.25)
  (print (dumps (c1.to-dict) :indent 2)))

(if (= --name-- "__main__")
  (main))

;; FIM Examples
;; ==========================================

<SUF>))
<MID>        [True (+ (fibonacci (- n 1)) (fibonacci (- n 2)))]

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Hypertalk Real Idiomatic Code
# ==========================================

(defn factorial [n]
  (if (<= n 1)
    1
    (* n (factorial (- n 1)))))

(defn filter-positive [coll]
  (filter #(pos? %) coll))

(defn sum-list [numbers]
  (reduce + 0 numbers))

<PRE>
(defn square [x]
<SUF>
)
<MID>
  (* x x)
<EOT>

fn_factorial_icon = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_icon(n - 1);
}

    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];

    return sum;

    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Protocol Buffers v3 & gRPC Interface Definition Language (IDL)
// ==========================================

syntax = "proto3";

package llpt.code.nano.v1;

option go_package = "github.com/llpt/code/nano/v1;nanov1";
option java_multiple_files = true;
option java_package = "com.llpt.code.nano.v1";

enum UserRole {
  USER_ROLE_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  USER_ROLE_GUEST = 1;
  USER_ROLE_MEMBER = 2;
  USER_ROLE_ADMIN = 3;
}

message User {
  int64 id = 1;
  string username = 2;
  string email = 3;
  UserRole role = 4;
  double balance = 5;
  repeated string tags = 6;
  map<string, string> metadata = 7;
}

message CreateUserRequest {
  string username = 1;
  string email = 2;
  UserRole role = 3;
}

message CreateUserResponse {
  User user = 1;
}

message GetUserRequest {
  int64 id = 1;
}

message StreamUserEventsRequest {
  int64 user_id = 1;
}

message UserEvent {
  int64 event_id = 1;
  string event_type = 2;
  string payload_json = 3;
}

// gRPC Service Definition
service UserService {
  rpc CreateUser(CreateUserRequest) returns (CreateUserResponse);
  rpc GetUser(GetUserRequest) returns (User);
  rpc StreamEvents(StreamUserEventsRequest) returns (stream UserEvent);
}

// FIM Examples

<MID>  double unit_price = 3;

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Io Real Idiomatic Code

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Io

fn_factorial_io = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;

def greet(name: str) -> str:
    return f"Hello, {name} from IronPython!"

def binary_search(arr: list, target: int) -> int:
    low, high = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        elif arr[mid] < target:
            low = mid + 1
        else:
            high = mid - 1
    return -1

// Java Dataset - Modern Java 17+ OOP, Streams, Concurrency & Data Modeling

package com.llpt.code;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import java.time.Instant;

// ==========================================
// 1. Immutable Records & Enums
// ==========================================

public class JavaDatasetExamples {

    public enum Role {
        ADMIN, DEVELOPER, DESIGNER, GUEST
    }

    public record User(
        String id,
        String name,
        String email,
        Role role,
        boolean active,
        Instant createdAt
    ) {
        public User {
            Objects.requireNonNull(id, "ID cannot be null");
            Objects.requireNonNull(email, "Email cannot be null");
        }

        public boolean isAdmin() {
            return role == Role.ADMIN;
        }
    }

    // Custom Exception
    public static class UserNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public UserNotFoundException(String id) {
            super("User not found with ID: " + id);
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 2. Stream API & Data Processing
    // ==========================================

    public static class UserService {
        private final Map<String, User> userDatabase = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

        public void saveUser(User user) {
            userDatabase.put(user.id(), user);
        }

        public Optional<User> findById(String id) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(userDatabase.get(id));
        }

        public List<User> filterActiveByRole(Role role) {
            return userDatabase.values().stream()
                .filter(User::active)
                .filter(u -> u.role() == role)
                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(User::name))
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        }

        public Map<Role, List<User>> groupUsersByRole() {
            return userDatabase.values().stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(User::role));
        }

        public double calculateActiveUserRatio() {
            if (userDatabase.isEmpty()) return 0.0;
            long activeCount = userDatabase.values().stream()
                .filter(User::active)
                .count();
            return (double) activeCount / userDatabase.size();
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 3. Asynchronous Execution & CompletableFuture
    // ==========================================

    public static class AsyncDataFetcher {
        private final ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(4);

        public CompletableFuture<String> fetchDataAsync(String endpoint) {
            return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(200); // Simulate network latency
                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    throw new IllegalStateException("Thread interrupted", e);
                }
                return "Response from " + endpoint;
            }, executor);
        }

        public CompletableFuture<List<String>> fetchAllEndpoints(List<String> endpoints) {
            List<CompletableFuture<String>> futures = endpoints.stream()
                .map(this::fetchDataAsync)
                .toList();

            CompletableFuture<Void> allOf = CompletableFuture.allOf(
                futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0])
            );

            return allOf.thenApply(v ->
                futures.stream()
                    .map(CompletableFuture::join)
                    .collect(Collectors.toList())
            );
        }

        public void shutdown() {
            executor.shutdown();
        }
    }

    // ==========================================
    // 4. Main Demonstration
    // ==========================================

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        UserService service = new UserService();
        User u1 = new User("1", "Alice", "alice@example.com", Role.ADMIN, true, Instant.now());
        User u2 = new User("2", "Bob", "bob@example.com", Role.DEVELOPER, true, Instant.now());
        User u3 = new User("3", "Charlie", "charlie@example.com", Role.DEVELOPER, false, Instant.now());

        service.saveUser(u1);
        service.saveUser(u2);
        service.saveUser(u3);

        List<User> developers = service.filterActiveByRole(Role.DEVELOPER);
        System.out.println("Active developers count: " + developers.size());

        AsyncDataFetcher fetcher = new AsyncDataFetcher();
        fetcher.fetchAllEndpoints(List.of("/api/v1/users", "/api/v1/orders"))
            .thenAccept(results -> results.forEach(System.out::println))
            .join();

        fetcher.shutdown();
    }
}


// ==========================================
// 4. Concurrent Thread Pool & Reactive Pattern
// ==========================================

import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;

public class ThreadPoolManager {
    private final ExecutorService executor;
    private final AtomicInteger taskCount = new AtomicInteger(0);

    public ThreadPoolManager(int poolSize) {
        this.executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(poolSize, new ThreadFactory() {
            private int count = 0;
            @Override
            public Thread newThread(Runnable r) {
                return new Thread(r, "CustomWorker-" + (++count));
            }
        });
    }

    public Future<String> submitTask(String taskName, long durationMs) {
        taskCount.incrementAndGet();
        return executor.submit(() -> {
            Thread.sleep(durationMs);
            return "Task " + taskName + " completed by " + Thread.currentThread().getName();
        });
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        executor.shutdown();
        try {
            if (!executor.awaitTermination(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                executor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            executor.shutdownNow();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}

// 2. Java 21 Virtual Threads & Structured Concurrency
// ==========================================

import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.IntStream;

public class VirtualThreadTaskProcessor {

    public static void processTasksInParallel() {
        try (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
            List<Future<String>> futures = IntStream.range(0, 100)
                .mapToObj(i -> executor.submit(() -> {
                    Thread.sleep(100);
                    return "Result from Virtual Thread " + i;
                }))
                .toList();

            for (var future : futures) {
                System.out.println(future.get());
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
}


// Java Concurrent Bounded Buffer Example

package com.llpt.nano;

import java.util.concurrent.locks.Condition;
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;

public class BoundedBuffer<T> {
    private final Object[] items;
    private final ReentrantLock lock = new ReentrantLock();
    private final Condition notFull  = lock.newCondition();
    private final Condition notEmpty = lock.newCondition();

    private int putptr, takeptr, count;

    public BoundedBuffer(int capacity) {
        items = new Object[capacity];
    }

    public void put(T x) throws InterruptedException {
        lock.lock();
        try {
            while (count == items.length)
                notFull.await();
            items[putptr] = x;
            if (++putptr == items.length) putptr = 0;
            ++count;
            notEmpty.signal();
        } finally {
            lock.unlock();
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public T take() throws InterruptedException {
        lock.lock();
        try {
            while (count == 0)
                notEmpty.await();
            T x = (T) items[takeptr];
            if (++takeptr == items.length) takeptr = 0;
            --count;
            notFull.signal();
            return x;
        } finally {
            lock.unlock();
        }
    }
}

package com.llpt.nano.extensions;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class StreamUtils {

    public static <T> List<T> filterAndSort(List<T> input, java.util.function.Predicate<T> predicate, java.util.Comparator<T> comparator) {
        return input.stream()
            .filter(predicate)
            .sorted(comparator)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
}

// JavaScript Dataset - Modern ES6+ Code Examples & Async Utilities

// ==========================================
// 1. Array Operations & Functional Programming
// ==========================================

const users = [
  { id: 1, name: 'Alice', age: 28, role: 'admin', active: true },
  { id: 2, name: 'Bob', age: 34, role: 'developer', active: true },
  { id: 3, name: 'Charlie', age: 22, role: 'designer', active: false },
  { id: 4, name: 'Diana', age: 31, role: 'developer', active: true }
];

function getActiveDevelopers(userList) {
  return userList
    .filter(u => u.active && u.role === 'developer')
    .map(u => ({ id: u.id, name: u.name, uppercaseName: u.name.toUpperCase() }));
}

function calculateAverageAge(userList) {
  if (!userList.length) return 0;
  const total = userList.reduce((acc, user) => acc + user.age, 0);
  return total / userList.length;
}

function groupByRole(userList) {
  return userList.reduce((acc, user) => {
    const role = user.role;
    if (!acc[role]) acc[role] = [];
    acc[role].push(user);
    return acc;
  }, {});
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Async/Await, Fetch API & Retry Logic
// ==========================================

async function fetchWithRetry(url, options = {}, retries = 3, backoffMs = 500) {
  for (let i = 0; i < retries; i++) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url, options);
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`HTTP Error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
      }
      return await response.json();
    } catch (err) {
      if (i === retries - 1) throw err;
      console.warn(`Request failed. Retrying in ${backoffMs}ms... (Attempt ${i + 1}/${retries})`);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, backoffMs));
      backoffMs *= 2;
    }
  }
}

// ==========================================
// 3. Event Emitter Pattern
// ==========================================

class EventEmitter {
  constructor() {
    this.events = new Map();
  }

  on(event, listener) {
    if (!this.events.has(event)) {
      this.events.set(event, []);
    }
    this.events.get(event).push(listener);
    return () => this.off(event, listener);
  }

  off(event, listenerToRemove) {
    if (!this.events.has(event)) return;
    const filtered = this.events.get(event).filter(l => l !== listenerToRemove);
    this.events.set(event, filtered);
  }

  emit(event, ...args) {
    if (!this.events.has(event)) return;
    this.events.get(event).forEach(listener => {
      try {
        listener(...args);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Error in event listener for '${event}':`, err);
      }
    });
  }
}

// ==========================================
// 4. Closures & Debounce/Throttle
// ==========================================

function debounce(func, wait) {
  let timeoutId = null;
  return function (...args) {
    const context = this;
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      func.apply(context, args);
    }, wait);
  };
}

function throttle(func, limit) {
  let inThrottle = false;
  return function (...args) {
    const context = this;
    if (!inThrottle) {
      func.apply(context, args);
      inThrottle = true;
      setTimeout(() => { inThrottle = false; }, limit);
    }
  };
}

// ==========================================
// 5. Utility Storage Wrapper & DOM Helpers
// ==========================================

class StorageHelper {
  static setJSON(key, value) {
    try {
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(value);
      localStorage.setItem(key, serialized);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to set localStorage key:', key, e);
    }
  }

  static getJSON(key, defaultValue = null) {
    try {
      const item = localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : defaultValue;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to parse localStorage key:', key, e);
      return defaultValue;
    }
  }
}

// Export / Execution
if (typeof module !== 'undefined') {
  module.exports = {
    getActiveDevelopers,
    calculateAverageAge,
    groupByRole,
    fetchWithRetry,
    EventEmitter,
    debounce,
    throttle,
    StorageHelper
  };
}


// ==========================================
// 4. Async Event Emitter & Middleware Pipeline
// ==========================================

class AsyncEventEmitter {
    constructor() {
        this.listeners = new Map();
    }

    on(event, fn) {
        if (!this.listeners.has(event)) {
            this.listeners.set(event, []);
        }
        this.listeners.get(event).push(fn);
        return () => this.off(event, fn);
    }

    off(event, fn) {
        const list = this.listeners.get(event);
        if (!list) return;
        this.listeners.set(event, list.filter(listener => listener !== fn));
    }

    async emit(event, ...args) {
        const list = this.listeners.get(event) || [];
        for (const listener of list) {
            await listener(...args);
        }
    }
}

class Pipeline {
    constructor() {
        this.middlewares = [];
    }

    use(fn) {
        this.middlewares.push(fn);
        return this;
    }

    async execute(context) {
        let index = -1;
        const dispatch = async (i) => {
            if (i <= index) throw new Error("next() called multiple times");
            index = i;
            const fn = this.middlewares[i];
            if (fn) {
                await fn(context, () => dispatch(i + 1));
            }
        };
        await dispatch(0);
        return context;
    }
}

// Example usage of Pipeline
const appPipeline = new Pipeline();
appPipeline.use(async (ctx, next) => {
    ctx.startTime = Date.now();
    await next();
    ctx.duration = Date.now() - ctx.startTime;
});

// File: src/services/ApiClient.js
class ApiClient {
    constructor(baseURL) {
        this.baseURL = baseURL;
    }

    async get(endpoint) {
        const response = await fetch(`${this.baseURL}${endpoint}`);
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`HTTP Error: ${response.status}`);
        }
        return await response.json();
    }
}

// File: src/components/UserWidget.js
class UserWidget {
    constructor(containerId, apiClient) {
        this.container = document.getElementById(containerId);
        this.apiClient = apiClient;
    }

    async render(userId) {
        try {
            this.container.innerHTML = "<p>Loading...</p>";
            const user = await this.apiClient.get(`/users/${userId}`);
            this.container.innerHTML = `<div class="user-card"><h3>${user.name}</h3><p>${user.email}</p></div>`;
        } catch (err) {
            this.container.innerHTML = `<p class="error">Error: ${err.message}</p>`;
        }
    }
}

# ==========================================
# 6. Instruction & Response Example
# ==========================================

# Instruction: Create a JavaScript memoization decorator function to cache expensive calculation results.
# Response:
function memoize(fn) {
    const cache = new Map();
    return function (...args) {
        const key = JSON.stringify(args);
        if (cache.has(key)) {
            return cache.get(key);
        }
        const result = fn.apply(this, args);
        cache.set(key, result);
        return result;
    };
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Node.js Event Loop & Custom Event Emitter
// ==========================================

class EventEmitter {
    constructor() {
        this.events = {};
    }

    on(event, listener) {
        if (!this.events[event]) {
            this.events[event] = [];
        }
        this.events[event].push(listener);
    }

    emit(event, ...args) {
        if (this.events[event]) {
            this.events[event].forEach(listener => listener(...args));
        }
    }

    off(event, listenerToRemove) {
        if (!this.events[event]) return;
        this.events[event] = this.events[event].filter(listener => listener !== listenerToRemove);
    }
}


// Modern JavaScript Async Iterator and Event Emitter

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';

export class TaskQueue extends EventEmitter {
  constructor(concurrency = 2) {
    super();
    this.concurrency = concurrency;
    this.running = 0;
    this.queue = [];
  }

  push(task) {
    this.queue.push(task);
    this.next();
  }

  async next() {
    if (this.running >= this.concurrency || this.queue.length === 0) return;
    this.running++;
    const task = this.queue.shift();
    try {
      const result = await task();
      this.emit('taskSuccess', result);
    } catch (err) {
      this.emit('taskError', err);
    } finally {
      this.running--;
      this.next();
    }
  }
}

fn_factorial_jolie = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_jolie(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_jolie = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_jolie = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

-- ==========================================

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

SELECT username, COUNT(*) as activity_count
FROM users u
JOIN logs l ON u.id = l.user_id
WHERE l.created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
GROUP BY username
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5
ORDER BY activity_count DESC;

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Julia Scientific Computing & High-Performance Data Science
# ==========================================

module LLPTScientific

using LinearAlgebra
using Statistics

export MatrixSolver, solve_system, calculate_statistics

struct MatrixSolver{T<:AbstractFloat}
    A::Matrix{T}
    b::Vector{T}

    function MatrixSolver(A::Matrix{T}, b::Vector{T}) where T<:AbstractFloat
        size(A, 1) == size(A, 2) || error("Matrix A must be square")
        size(A, 1) == length(b) || error("Dimension mismatch between A and b")
        new{T}(A, b)
    end
end

# Multiple Dispatch
function solve_system(solver::MatrixSolver{T})::Vector{T} where T
    return solver.A \ solver.b
end

function calculate_statistics(data::AbstractArray{T})::NamedTuple where T<:Real
    m = mean(data)
    s = std(data)
    med = median(data)
    return (mean=m, std=s, median=med)
end

# Parallelization & Macro Vectorization
function fast_vector_dot(x::Vector{Float64}, y::Vector{Float64})::Float64
    @assert length(x) == length(y)
    acc = 0.0
    @simd for i in 1:length(x)
        @inbounds acc += x[i] * y[i]
    end
    return acc
end

end # module

    replicas: int = 1
    containers: [Container]

myDeployment = Deployment {
    name = "llpt-nano"

            name = "nano-api"

### LINGUAGEM: Kotlin
### ID: kotlin | Seção 189/405
# ======================================================================

// Kotlin Dataset - Modern Idiomatic Kotlin, Coroutines & Functional Code

package com.llpt.code

import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.*
import java.time.Instant

// ==========================================
// 1. Data Classes & Sealed Interfaces
// ==========================================

enum class UserRole { ADMIN, DEVELOPER, GUEST }

data class User(
    val id: String,
    val username: String,
    val email: String,
    val role: UserRole = UserRole.GUEST,
    val isActive: Boolean = true,
    val createdAt: Instant = Instant.now()
)

sealed interface UIState<out T> {
    data object Idle : UIState<Nothing>
    data object Loading : UIState<Nothing>
    data class Success<T>(val data: T) : UIState<T>
    data class Error(val message: String, val cause: Throwable? = null) : UIState<Nothing>
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Extension Functions & Scope Functions
// ==========================================

fun String.isValidEmail(): Boolean {
    val emailRegex = "^[A-Za-z0-9+_.-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+$"
    return this.matches(emailRegex.toRegex())
}

fun List<User>.filterActiveDevelopers(): List<User> = this
    .filter { it.isActive && it.role == UserRole.DEVELOPER }
    .sortedBy { it.username }

fun User.toSummaryString(): String = run {
    "[User #$id] $username ($email) - Role: $role | Active: $isActive"
}

// ==========================================
// 3. Repository & Coroutines Flow
// ==========================================

interface UserRepository {
    fun getUserStream(): Flow<UIState<List<User>>>
    suspend fun getUserById(id: String): UIState<User>
    suspend fun saveUser(user: User): Result<User>
}

class InMemoryUserRepository : UserRepository {
    private val usersMap = mutableMapOf<String, User>()
    private val _userFlow = MutableStateFlow<UIState<List<User>>>(UIState.Idle)

    override fun getUserStream(): Flow<UIState<List<User>>> = _userFlow.asStateFlow()

    override suspend fun getUserById(id: String): UIState<User> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val user = usersMap[id]
        if (user != null) {
            UIState.Success(user)
        } else {
            UIState.Error("User with id $id not found")
        }
    }

    override suspend fun saveUser(user: User): Result<User> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        runCatching {
            require(user.email.isValidEmail()) { "Invalid email address format: ${user.email}" }
            usersMap[user.id] = user
            _userFlow.value = UIState.Success(usersMap.values.toList())
            user
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 4. Coroutines Concurrent Fetching
// ==========================================

class DataAggregator(private val repository: UserRepository) {

    suspend fun aggregateUserData(userIds: List<String>): List<User> = coroutineScope {
        userIds.map { id ->
            async {
                when (val state = repository.getUserById(id)) {
                    is UIState.Success -> state.data
                    else -> null
                }
            }
        }.awaitAll().filterNotNull()
    }
}

suspend fun main() = coroutineScope {
    val repo = InMemoryUserRepository()
    val u1 = User(id = "1", username = "alice", email = "alice@example.com", role = UserRole.ADMIN)
    val u2 = User(id = "2", username = "bob", email = "bob@example.com", role = UserRole.DEVELOPER)

    repo.saveUser(u1)
    repo.saveUser(u2)

    val aggregator = DataAggregator(repo)
    val users = aggregator.aggregateUserData(listOf("1", "2", "3"))
    println("Aggregated Users Count: ${users.size}")

    users.forEach { user ->
        println(user.toSummaryString())
    }
}


// ==========================================
// 4. Coroutines Flow & Channel Pattern
// ==========================================

import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.Channel

class DataStreamRepository {
    fun fetchNumbersStream(): Flow<Int> = flow {
        for (i in 1..5) {
            delay(100)
            emit(i * 10)
        }
    }.flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)

    suspend fun processChannelCommunication() = coroutineScope {
        val channel = Channel<String>()
        launch {
            channel.send("Event 1")
            channel.send("Event 2")
            channel.close()
        }

        for (msg in channel) {
            println("Received channel message: $msg")
        }
    }
}

// Instruction: Write a thread-safe Singleton in Kotlin using lazy delegate and state handling.
// Response:
class DatabaseManager private constructor() {
    init {
        println("DatabaseManager initialized.")
    }

    fun query(sql: String): List<String> {
        return listOf("Row 1 for: $sql", "Row 2 for: $sql")
    }

    companion object {
        val instance: DatabaseManager by lazy(LazyThreadSafetyMode.SYNCHRONIZED) {
            DatabaseManager()
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Kotlin Coroutines & Flow Reactive Streams
// ==========================================

import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.*

fun fetchNumbersFlow(): Flow<Int> = flow {
    for (i in 1..5) {
        delay(100)
        emit(i)
    }
}

suspend fn processCoroutinesFlow() = coroutineScope {
    fetchNumbersFlow()
        .map { it * 2 }
        .filter { it > 4 }
        .collect { value ->
            println("Collected: $value")
        }
}
// ==========================================
// Native Kotlin Extension Functions
// ==========================================

package com.llpt.nano.extensions

inline fun <T, R> Iterable<T>.mapNonNull(transform: (T) -> R?): List<R> {
    val destination = ArrayList<R>()
    for (item in this) {
        val result = transform(item)
        if (result != null) {
            destination.add(result)
        }
    }
    return destination
}

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Kotlin Multiplatform (KMP), Coroutines & Flow
// ==========================================

package com.llpt.code.nano

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filter
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay

enum class UserStatus {
    ACTIVE, PENDING, SUSPENDED
}

data class User(
    val id: Long,
    val name: String,
    val email: String,
    val status: UserStatus = UserStatus.ACTIVE,
    val balance: Double = 0.0
)

interface UserRepository {
    suspend fun getUserById(id: Long): User?
    fun observeActiveUsers(): Flow<List<User>>
    suspend fun updateUser(user: User): Boolean
}

class DefaultUserRepository : UserRepository {

    private val userDatabase = mutableMapOf<Long, User>()

    override suspend fun getUserById(id: Long): User? {
        delay(50) // Simulating network latency
        return userDatabase[id]
    }

    override fun observeActiveUsers(): Flow<List<User>> = flow {
        while (true) {
            val activeUsers = userDatabase.values.filter { it.status == UserStatus.ACTIVE }
            emit(activeUsers)
            delay(1000)
        }
    }

    override suspend fun updateUser(user: User): Boolean {
        delay(20)
        userDatabase[user.id] = user
        return true
    }
}

// Extension Functions & KMP Expect/Actual
expect class PlatformLogger() {
    fun log(message: String)
}

fun List<User>.calculateTotalBalance(): Double {
    return this.fold(0.0) { acc, user -> acc + user.balance }
}

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Kotlin Native

fn_factorial_kotlin_native = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_kotlin_native(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_kotlin_native = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_kotlin_native = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

-- ==========================================
-- LLPT Code Nano - Lean 4 Interactive Theorem Prover & Functional Code
-- ==========================================

import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Basic

-- Natural Number Addition Proof
theorem add_zero_eq (n : Nat) : n + 0 = n := by
  rfl

theorem add_succ_eq (n m : Nat) : n + Nat.succ m = Nat.succ (n + m) := by
  rfl

-- Recursive Function Definition
def fibonacci : Nat → Nat
  | 0 => 0
  | 1 => 1
  | n + 2 => fibonacci (n + 1) + fibonacci n

-- Verification Property
theorem fib_zero : fibonacci 0 = 0 := by rfl
theorem fib_one : fibonacci 1 = 1 := by rfl
theorem fib_two : fibonacci 2 = 1 := by rfl

-- Custom Inductive Structure
inductive ListTree (α : Type) where
  | nil : ListTree α
  | node (val : α) (children : List (ListTree α)) : ListTree α

-- FIM Examples
-- ==========================================

<SUF>
<MID>  n + n
<EOT>

### LINGUAGEM: Llvm Ir
### ID: llvm_ir | Seção 203/405
# ======================================================================

; ==========================================
; LLPT Code Nano - LLVM Intermediate Representation (IR)
; ==========================================

target datalayout = "e-m:e-p270:32:32-p271:32:32-p272:64:64-i64:64-f80:128-n8:16:32:64-S128"
target triple = "x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu"

%struct.Vector3 = type { float, float, float }

; Function: Add two floats
define float @add_floats(float %a, float %b) #0 {
entry:
  %res = fadd float %a, %b
  ret float %res
}

; Function: Factorial (Loop in LLVM IR)
define i64 @factorial(i64 %n) #0 {
entry:
  %cmp = icmp sle i64 %n, 1
  br i1 %cmp, label %if.then, label %if.else

if.then:
  ret i64 1

if.else:
  %n.sub = sub nsw i64 %n, 1
  %call = call i64 @factorial(i64 %n.sub)
  %mult = mul nsw i64 %n, %call
  ret i64 %mult
}

; Function: Vector3 Dot Product
define float @vector3_dot(%struct.Vector3* %v1, %struct.Vector3* %v2) #0 {
entry:
  %x1.ptr = getelementptr inbounds %struct.Vector3, %struct.Vector3* %v1, i32 0, i32 0
  %x1 = load float, float* %x1.ptr, align 4
  %x2.ptr = getelementptr inbounds %struct.Vector3, %struct.Vector3* %v2, i32 0, i32 0
  %x2 = load float, float* %x2.ptr, align 4
  %prod.x = fmul float %x1, %x2

  %y1.ptr = getelementptr inbounds %struct.Vector3, %struct.Vector3* %v1, i32 0, i32 1
  %y1 = load float, float* %y1.ptr, align 4
  %y2.ptr = getelementptr inbounds %struct.Vector3, %struct.Vector3* %v2, i32 0, i32 1
  %y2 = load float, float* %y2.ptr, align 4
  %prod.y = fmul float %y1, %y2

  %sum = fadd float %prod.x, %prod.y
  ret float %sum
}

attributes #0 = { nounwind sspreq uwtable }

; ==========================================
; FIM Examples
; ==========================================
<PRE>define i32 @square(i32 %x) {
entry:
<SUF>  ret i32 %res
}
<MID>  %res = mul i32 %x, %x
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Llvm Ir

# Lox Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

# ==========================================
# 1. Functions and Data Structures
# ==========================================

def greet(name: str) -> str:
    return f"Hello, {name} from Lox!"

def binary_search(arr: list, target: int) -> int:
    low, high = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        elif arr[mid] < target:
            low = mid + 1
        else:
            high = mid - 1
    return -1

# ==========================================
# 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
# ==========================================
<PRE>
def factorial(n: int) -> int:
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
<SUF>
<MID>
    return n * factorial(n - 1)
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Lox

### LINGUAGEM: Lua Luau
### ID: lua_luau | Seção 209/405
# ======================================================================

-- ==========================================
-- LLPT Code Nano - Luau Typed Lua Engine (Roblox & Luau)
-- ==========================================

export type UserStatus = "Active" | "Pending" | "Suspended"

export type User = {
    id: number,
    name: string,
    email: string,
    status: UserStatus,
    balance: number,
}

local UserModule = {}
UserModule.__index = UserModule

function UserModule.new(id: number, name: string, email: string): User
    local self: User = {
        id = id,
        name = name,
        email = email,
        status = "Active",
        balance = 0.0,
    }
    return self
end

function UserModule.credit(user: User, amount: number): number
    assert(amount > 0, "Credit amount must be positive")
    user.balance += amount
    return user.balance
end

function UserModule.debit(user: User, amount: number): (boolean, string?)
    if amount > user.balance then
        return false, "Insufficient balance"
    end
    user.balance -= amount
    return true, nil
end

-- Luau Parallel Luau & Task Library
local task = task or { spawn = function(f) coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(f)) end }

function UserModule.batchProcessUsers(users: { User })
    for _, usr in users do
        task.spawn(function()
            print("Processing user in Luau task:", usr.name, usr.email)
        end)
    end
end

return UserModule

-- ==========================================
-- FIM Examples
-- ==========================================
<PRE>local function calculateTax(amount: number): number
    if amount <= 0 then return 0 end
<SUF>    return tax
end
<MID>    local tax = amount * 0.15
<EOT>

# 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Lustre

fn_factorial_lustre = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_lustre(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_lustre = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

# 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_lustre = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

% ==========================================
% LLPT Code Nano - MATLAB Matrix Operations & Control Systems
% ==========================================

classdef SignalFilter < handle
    properties (Access = private)
        SamplingRate (1,1) double
        CutoffFreq (1,1) double
        FilterOrder (1,1) double
    end

    methods
        function obj = SignalFilter(Fs, Fc, Order)
            obj.SamplingRate = Fs;
            obj.CutoffFreq = Fc;
            obj.FilterOrder = Order;
        end

        function filtered_signal = apply_butterworth(obj, input_signal)
            Wn = obj.CutoffFreq / (obj.SamplingRate / 2);
            [b, a] = butter(obj.FilterOrder, Wn, 'low');
            filtered_signal = filter(b, a, input_signal);
        end

        function [f, P1] = compute_fft(obj, signal)
            L = length(signal);
            Y = fft(signal);
            P2 = abs(Y / L);
            P1 = P2(1:floor(L/2)+1);
            P1(2:end-1) = 2 * P1(2:end-1);
            f = obj.SamplingRate * (0:(L/2)) / L;
        end
    end
end

% ODE Solver Function
function dydt = pendulum_ode(t, y, L, g)
    theta = y(1);
    omega = y(2);
    dydt = [omega; -(g/L) * sin(theta)];
end

% FIM Examples

        result = eig(A);

% Mercury Logic Programming Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

% ==========================================
% 1. Facts and Rules
% ==========================================

parent(albert, bob).
parent(bob, charlie).

ancestor(X, Y) :- parent(X, Y).
ancestor(X, Y) :- parent(X, Z), ancestor(Z, Y).

factorial(0, 1).
factorial(N, F) :-
    N > 0,
    N1 is N - 1,
    factorial(N1, F1),
    F is N * F1.

% ==========================================
% 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
% ==========================================
<PRE>
append([], L, L).
<SUF>
<MID>
append([H|T], L, [H|R]) :-
    append(T, L, R).
<EOT>
