#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Shell Dataset - Modern Robust Bash Scripting, Automation & Systems Management

set -euo pipefail
IFS=$'\n\t'

# ==========================================
# 1. Colors & Logging Utility Functions
# ==========================================

readonly RED='\033[0;31m'
readonly GREEN='\033[0;32m'
readonly YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
readonly BLUE='\033[0;34m'
readonly NC='\033[0m' # No Color

log_info() {
    echo -e "${GREEN}[INFO]$(date +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'):${NC} $1"
}

log_warn() {
    echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN]$(date +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'):${NC} $1" >&2
}

log_error() {
    echo -e "${RED}[ERROR]$(date +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'):${NC} $1" >&2
}

# Cleanup on exit
cleanup() {
    local exit_code=$?
    if [ $exit_code -ne 0 ]; then
        log_error "Script failed with exit code ${exit_code}."
    else
        log_info "Script completed successfully."
    fi
    # Remove temporary directory if exists
    if [ -n "${TMP_DIR:-}" ] && [ -d "${TMP_DIR}" ]; then
        rm -rf "${TMP_DIR}"
    fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT

# ==========================================
# 2. Dependency Checks & Helper Functions
# ==========================================

check_command() {
    local cmd=$1
    if ! command -v "${cmd}" &> /dev/null; then
        log_error "Required command '${cmd}' is not installed or not in PATH."
        exit 1
    fi
}

check_command "curl"
check_command "jq"
check_command "tar"

# ==========================================
# 3. CLI Option Parsing (getopts)
# ==========================================

usage() {
    cat <<EOF
Usage: $(basename "$0") [OPTIONS]

Options:
  -d, --directory DIR    Target directory to backup (Required)
  -o, --output DIR       Backup destination folder (Default: /tmp/backups)
  -v, --verbose          Enable verbose output
  -h, --help             Show this help message and exit
EOF
    exit 1
}

TARGET_DIR=""
OUTPUT_DIR="/tmp/backups"
VERBOSE=false

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
    case $1 in
        -d|--directory)
            TARGET_DIR="$2"
            shift 2
            ;;
        -o|--output)
            OUTPUT_DIR="$2"
            shift 2
            ;;
        -v|--verbose)
            VERBOSE=true
            shift
            ;;
        -h|--help)
            usage
            ;;
        *)
            log_error "Unknown argument: $1"
            usage
            ;;
    esac
done

if [ -z "${TARGET_DIR}" ]; then
    log_error "Missing required argument: --directory"
    usage
fi

# ==========================================
# 4. Backup Operation
# ==========================================

perform_backup() {
    local src=$1
    local dest=$2

    if [ ! -d "${src}" ]; then
        log_error "Source directory '${src}' does not exist."
        exit 1
    fi

    mkdir -p "${dest}"
    TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

    local timestamp
    timestamp=$(date +'%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    local archive_name="backup_${timestamp}.tar.gz"
    local archive_path="${dest}/${archive_name}"

    log_info "Creating archive '${archive_path}' from '${src}'..."

    if [ "${VERBOSE}" = true ]; then
        tar -czvf "${archive_path}" -C "$(dirname "${src}")" "$(basename "${src}")"
    else
        tar -czf "${archive_path}" -C "$(dirname "${src}")" "$(basename "${src}")"
    fi

    log_info "Backup archive created successfully at '${archive_path}'."
}

perform_backup "${TARGET_DIR}" "${OUTPUT_DIR}"


# ==========================================
# 4. Robust Log Rotation Script
# ==========================================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

LOG_DIR="/var/log/myapp"
BACKUP_DIR="/var/log/myapp/archive"
MAX_DAYS=7

mkdir -p "${BACKUP_DIR}"

find "${LOG_DIR}" -type f -name "*.log" -mtime +1 | while read -r logfile; do
    filename=$(basename "${logfile}")
    timestamp=$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)
    gzip -c "${logfile}" > "${BACKUP_DIR}/${filename}_${timestamp}.gz"
    > "${logfile}"
    echo "Archived ${logfile}"
done

find "${BACKUP_DIR}" -type f -name "*.gz" -mtime +${MAX_DAYS} -delete

# ==========================================
# 2. Robust Bash Error Handling & Options
# ==========================================

set -euo pipefail

trap 'echo "Erro na linha $LINENO. Abortando script..."' ERR

log_info() {
    echo "[INFO] $(date +'%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'): $1"
}

log_info "Iniciando processamento com segurança..."
# ==========================================
# Native POSIX Shell File Processing
# ==========================================

count_lines_in_files() {
    local target_dir="$1"
    if [ -d "$target_dir" ]; then
        find "$target_dir" -type f -name "*.txt" | wc -l
    fi
}

Object subclass: #LLPTCustomer
	instanceVariableNames: 'id name email balance status'
	classVariableNames: ''
	package: 'LLPT-Code-Nano'!

!LLPTCustomer methodsFor: 'initialization'!
initialize
	super initialize.
	balance := 0.0.
	status := #active.! !

!LLPTCustomer methodsFor: 'accessing'!
id: anInteger
	id := anInteger!

id
	^ id!

name: aString
	name := aString!

name
	^ name!

email: aString
	email := aString!

email
	^ email!

balance
	^ balance! !

!LLPTCustomer methodsFor: 'domain-logic'!
deposit: anAmount
	anAmount <= 0 ifTrue: [ Error signal: 'Amount must be positive' ].
	balance := balance + anAmount.
	^ balance!

withdraw: anAmount
	anAmount > balance ifTrue: [ Error signal: 'Insufficient funds' ].
	balance := balance - anAmount.
	^ balance! !

# ======================================================================
### LINGUAGEM: Solidity
### ID: solidity | Seção 337/405
# ======================================================================

// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Solidity 0.8.20 Smart Contract & Web3 Security
// ==========================================

pragma solidity ^0.8.20;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";

error InsufficientBalance(uint256 requested, uint256 available);
error TransferFailed();
error ZeroAddressNotAllowed();

contract LLPTTokenVault is ERC20, Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {

    mapping(address => uint256) private _stakedBalances;
    mapping(address => uint256) private _stakeTimestamps;

    uint256 public constant STAKING_REWARD_RATE = 10; // 10% APY
    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;

    event Staked(address indexed user, uint256 amount);
    event Unstaked(address indexed user, uint256 amount, uint256 reward);

    constructor(uint256 initialSupply) ERC20("LLPT Nano Token", "LLPTN") Ownable(msg.sender) {
        _mint(msg.sender, initialSupply * 10**decimals());
    }

    function stake(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {
        if (amount == 0) revert InsufficientBalance(amount, 0);
        if (balanceOf(msg.sender) < amount) revert InsufficientBalance(amount, balanceOf(msg.sender));

        _transfer(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
        _stakedBalances[msg.sender] += amount;
        _stakeTimestamps[msg.sender] = block.timestamp;

        emit Staked(msg.sender, amount);
    }

    function calculateReward(address user) public view returns (uint256) {
        uint256 staked = _stakedBalances[user];
        if (staked == 0) return 0;

        uint256 duration = block.timestamp - _stakeTimestamps[user];
        uint256 reward = (staked * STAKING_REWARD_RATE * duration) / (100 * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);
        return reward;
    }

    function unstake() external nonReentrant {
        uint256 staked = _stakedBalances[msg.sender];
        if (staked == 0) revert InsufficientBalance(0, 0);

        uint256 reward = calculateReward(msg.sender);
        _stakedBalances[msg.sender] = 0;

        _transfer(address(this), msg.sender, staked);
        if (reward > 0) {
            _mint(msg.sender, reward);
        }

        emit Unstaked(msg.sender, staked, reward);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>function withdraw(uint256 amount) external {
    require(balances[msg.sender] >= amount, "Insufficient balance");
    balances[msg.sender] -= amount;
<SUF>    require(success, "Transfer failed");
}
<MID>    (bool success, ) = payable(msg.sender).call{value: amount}("");
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Solidity

-- SPARQL Query Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

-- ==========================================
-- 1. Table Definitions and Queries
-- ==========================================

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

SELECT username, COUNT(*) as activity_count
FROM users u
JOIN logs l ON u.id = l.user_id
WHERE l.created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
GROUP BY username
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5
ORDER BY activity_count DESC;

-- ==========================================
-- 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
-- ==========================================
<PRE>
SELECT department_id, AVG(salary) as avg_sal
FROM employees
<SUF>
ORDER BY avg_sal DESC;
<MID>
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
GROUP BY department_id
<EOT>

# ======================================================================
### LINGUAGEM: SQL
### ID: sql | Seção 343/405
# ======================================================================

-- SQL Dataset - Advanced Relational Queries, CTEs, Window Functions & DDL Schemas

-- ==========================================
-- 1. Database Schema DDL Definitions
-- ==========================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roles (
    role_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    role_name VARCHAR(50) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    role_id INT NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_user_role FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES roles(role_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_logs (
    log_id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id UUID NOT NULL,
    action_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    metadata JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT fk_log_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- Optimization Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_users_email ON users(email);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_role_active ON users(role_id, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_logs_user_created ON activity_logs(user_id, created_at DESC);

-- ==========================================
-- 2. Common Table Expressions (CTEs) & Aggregations
-- ==========================================

WITH UserActivitySummary AS (
    SELECT
        u.user_id,
        u.username,
        u.email,
        r.role_name,
        COUNT(l.log_id) AS total_actions,
        MAX(l.created_at) AS last_active_at
    FROM users u
    INNER JOIN roles r ON u.role_id = r.role_id
    LEFT JOIN activity_logs l ON u.user_id = l.user_id
    WHERE u.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY u.user_id, u.username, u.email, r.role_name
)
SELECT
    user_id,
    username,
    email,
    role_name,
    total_actions,
    COALESCE(last_active_at, created_at) AS last_activity
FROM UserActivitySummary
WHERE total_actions >= 5
ORDER BY total_actions DESC, last_activity DESC;

-- ==========================================
-- 3. Window Functions (Partitioning & Ranking)
-- ==========================================

SELECT
    user_id,
    action_type,
    created_at,
    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY created_at DESC) AS action_rank,
    COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY user_id) AS user_total_logs
FROM activity_logs
WHERE created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
ORDER BY user_id, action_rank;

-- ==========================================
-- 4. Safe Transaction Example
-- ==========================================

BEGIN TRANSACTION;

UPDATE users
SET is_active = FALSE, updated_at = NOW()
WHERE user_id = 'a0eebc99-9c0b-4ef8-bb6d-6bb9bd380a11';

INSERT INTO activity_logs (user_id, action_type, metadata)
VALUES (
    'a0eebc99-9c0b-4ef8-bb6d-6bb9bd380a11',
    'ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED',
    '{"reason": "User request", "admin_id": "system"}'::jsonb
);

COMMIT;


-- ==========================================
-- 4. Window Functions & Recursive CTE
-- ==========================================

WITH RECURSIVE CategoryHierarchy AS (
    SELECT id, name, parent_id, 1 as depth
    FROM categories
    WHERE parent_id IS NULL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT c.id, c.name, c.parent_id, ch.depth + 1
    FROM categories c
    INNER JOIN CategoryHierarchy ch ON c.parent_id = ch.id
)
SELECT id, name, depth
FROM CategoryHierarchy
ORDER BY depth, name;

-- ==========================================
-- 2. Advanced CTE, Window Functions & Indexing
-- ==========================================

WITH RankedOrders AS (
    SELECT
        customer_id,
        order_id,
        total_amount,
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY customer_id ORDER BY total_amount DESC) as rank_num
    FROM orders
)
SELECT customer_id, order_id, total_amount
FROM RankedOrders
WHERE rank_num = 1;
-- ==========================================
-- Native SQL Common Table Expressions
-- ==========================================

WITH active_users AS (
    SELECT id, email, created_at
    FROM users
    WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
)
SELECT id, email FROM active_users;

-- SQL FIM Example
/*
<PRE>
SELECT category_id, COUNT(*) as total_items
FROM products
<SUF>
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5;
<MID>
GROUP BY category_id
<EOT>
*/

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Squeak

fn_factorial_squeak = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_squeak(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_squeak = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_squeak = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

### LINGUAGEM: Swift
### ID: swift | Seção 350/405

// Swift Dataset - Modern Swift Concurrency, Protocols, Enums & SwiftUI Architecture

import Foundation

// ==========================================
// 1. Enums with Associated Values & Protocols
// ==========================================

public enum UserRole: String, Codable, CaseIterable {
    case admin
    case developer
    case guest
}

public enum NetworkResult<T> {
    case success(T)
    case failure(Error)
}

public protocol IdentifiableEntity {
    var id: UUID { get }
    var createdAt: Date { get }
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Structs, Value Types & Codable
// ==========================================

public struct User: IdentifiableEntity, Codable, Equatable {
    public let id: UUID
    public var username: String
    public var email: String
    public var role: UserRole
    public var isActive: Bool
    public let createdAt: Date

    public init(
        id: UUID = UUID(),
        username: String,
        email: String,
        role: UserRole = .guest,
        isActive: Bool = true,
        createdAt: Date = Date()
    ) {
        self.id = id
        self.username = username
        self.email = email
        self.role = role
        self.isActive = isActive
        self.createdAt = createdAt
    }

    public mutating func deactivate() {
        self.isActive = false
    }
}

// Extension with Computed Properties & Utilities
extension User {
    public var isAdmin: Bool {
        return role == .admin
    }

    public var formattedSummary: String {
        return "[\(role.rawValue.uppercased())] \(username) (\(email)) - Active: \(isActive)"
    }
}

// Custom Error Type
public enum ServiceError: LocalizedError {
    case userNotFound(UUID)
    case invalidEmail(String)
    case networkFailure(String)

    public var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .userNotFound(let id):
            return "User with ID \(id) could not be found."
        case .invalidEmail(let email):
            return "The provided email address '\(email)' is invalid."
        case .networkFailure(let reason):
            return "Network operation failed: \(reason)."
        }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 3. Async/Await & Actor Concurrency
// ==========================================

public actor UserDatabaseStore {
    private var users: [UUID: User] = [:]

    public init() {}

    public func save(user: User) async throws {
        guard user.email.contains("@") else {
            throw ServiceError.invalidEmail(user.email)
        }
        users[user.id] = user
    }

    public func findUser(by id: UUID) async throws -> User {
        guard let user = users[id] else {
            throw ServiceError.userNotFound(id)
        }
        return user
    }

    public func fetchActiveDevelopers() async -> [User] {
        return users.values.filter { $0.isActive && $0.role == .developer }
            .sorted { $0.username < $1.username }
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 4. Usage Example
// ==========================================

@main
struct SwiftDatasetMain {
    static func main() async {
        print("=== Modern Swift Concurrency Examples ===")

        let store = UserDatabaseStore()
        let alice = User(username: "alice", email: "alice@swift.org", role: .admin)
        let bob = User(username: "bob", email: "bob@swift.org", role: .developer)

        do {
            try await store.save(user: alice)
            try await store.save(user: bob)

            let fetchedAlice = try await store.findUser(by: alice.id)
            print("Fetched User: \(fetchedAlice.formattedSummary)")

            let developers = await store.fetchActiveDevelopers()
            print("Active Developers Count: \(developers.count)")
        } catch {
            print("Execution failed with error: \(error.localizedDescription)")
        }
    }
}


// ==========================================
// 4. Swift Concurrency & Actor Model
// ==========================================

import Foundation

actor BankAccount {
    private(set) var balance: Double

    init(initialBalance: Double) {
        self.balance = initialBalance
    }

    func deposit(amount: Double) {
        balance += amount
    }

    func withdraw(amount: Double) throws {
        guard balance >= amount else {
            throw NSError(domain: "InsufficientFunds", code: 400)
        }
        balance -= amount
    }
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Swift Structured Concurrency & Actors
// ==========================================

actor BankAccount {
    private var balance: Double

    init(initialBalance: Double) {
        self.balance = initialBalance
    }

    func deposit(amount: Double) {
        balance += amount
    }

    func withdraw(amount: Double) throws -> Double {
        guard balance >= amount else {
            throw NSError(domain: "InsufficientFunds", code: 400)
        }
        balance -= amount
        return balance
    }
}
// ==========================================
// Native Swift 5.9 Functional Extensions
// ==========================================

extension Sequence {
    public func concurrentMap<T>(_ transform: @escaping (Element) async throws -> T) async rethrows -> [T] {
        let tasks = self.map { element in
            Task {
                try await transform(element)
            }
        }
        var results = [T]()
        for task in tasks {
            results.append(try await task.value)
        }
        return results
    }
}

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Hardware Description Languages (SystemVerilog & VHDL)
// ==========================================

// ------------------------------------------
// SystemVerilog: Pipelined FIFO Buffer
// ------------------------------------------
module fifo_buffer #(
    parameter int DATA_WIDTH = 32,
    parameter int DEPTH      = 16
)(
    input  logic                  clk,
    input  logic                  rst_n,
    input  logic                  wr_en,
    input  logic                  rd_en,
    input  logic [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] din,
    output logic [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] dout,
    output logic                  full,
    output logic                  empty
);

    localparam int ADDR_WIDTH = $clog2(DEPTH);

    logic [DATA_WIDTH-1:0] mem [DEPTH];
    logic [ADDR_WIDTH:0]   wr_ptr, rd_ptr;

    assign empty = (wr_ptr == rd_ptr);
    assign full  = (wr_ptr[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0] == rd_ptr[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]) &&
                   (wr_ptr[ADDR_WIDTH]     != rd_ptr[ADDR_WIDTH]);

    assign dout = mem[rd_ptr[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]];

    always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if (!rst_n) begin
            wr_ptr <= '0;
            rd_ptr <= '0;
        end else begin
            if (wr_en && !full) begin
                mem[wr_ptr[ADDR_WIDTH-1:0]] <= din;
                wr_ptr <= wr_ptr + 1'b1;
            end
            if (rd_en && !empty) begin
                rd_ptr <= rd_ptr + 1'b1;
            end
        end
    end

endmodule

// ------------------------------------------
// VHDL: 8-bit Counter Module
// ------------------------------------------
/*
library IEEE;
use IEEE.STD_LOGIC_1164.ALL;
use IEEE.NUMERIC_STD.ALL;

entity counter_8bit is
    Port ( clk   : in  STD_LOGIC;
           rst   : in  STD_LOGIC;
           count : out STD_LOGIC_VECTOR (7 downto 0));
end counter_8bit;

architecture Behavioral of counter_8bit is
    signal internal_count : unsigned(7 downto 0) := (others => '0');
begin
    process(clk, rst)
    begin
        if rst = '1' then
            internal_count <= (others => '0');
        elsif rising_edge(clk) then
            internal_count <= internal_count + 1;
        end if;
    end process;
    count <= std_logic_vector(internal_count);
end Behavioral;
*/

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>always_ff @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n)
        q <= '0;
<SUF>end
<MID>    else if (enable)
        q <= d;
<EOT>

-- T-SQL Query Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

-- ==========================================
-- 1. Table Definitions and Queries
-- ==========================================

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

SELECT username, COUNT(*) as activity_count
FROM users u
JOIN logs l ON u.id = l.user_id
WHERE l.created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
GROUP BY username
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5
ORDER BY activity_count DESC;

-- ==========================================
-- 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
-- ==========================================
<PRE>
SELECT department_id, AVG(salary) as avg_sal
FROM employees
<SUF>
ORDER BY avg_sal DESC;
<MID>
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
GROUP BY department_id
<EOT>

# ==========================================
# LLPT Code Nano - Tcl/Tk Scripting & GUI Engine
# ==========================================

package require Tk

# Namespace Isolation
namespace eval ::LLPTCustomer {
    variable balance 0.0
    variable status "Active"

    proc initGUI {} {
        wm title . "LLPT Code Nano - Tcl/Tk GUI"
        wm geometry . "400x250"

        label .lblTitle -text "Customer Management Panel" -font {Helvetica 14 bold}
        pack .lblTitle -pady 10

        frame .frmInput
        label .frmInput.lblAmount -text "Amount ($):"
        entry .frmInput.entAmount -textvariable ::LLPTCustomer::amountVar
        pack .frmInput.lblAmount .frmInput.entAmount -side left -padx 5
        pack .frmInput -pady 5

        label .lblBalance -text "Current Balance: $0.00" -font {Helvetica 12}
        pack .lblBalance -pady 10

        button .btnDeposit -text "Deposit" -command {::LLPTCustomer::deposit}
        pack .btnDeposit -side left -padx 20 -expand yes
    }

    proc deposit {} {
        variable balance
        variable amountVar
        if {[string is double -strict $amountVar] && $amountVar > 0} {
            set balance [expr {$balance + $amountVar}]
            .lblBalance configure -text [format "Current Balance: $%.2f" $balance]
            set amountVar ""
        } else {
            tk_messageBox -icon error -message "Please enter a valid positive deposit amount."
        }
    }
}

::LLPTCustomer::initGUI

# ==========================================
# FIM Examples
# ==========================================
<PRE>proc calculate_factorial {n} {
    if {$n <= 1} {
        return 1
    }
<SUF>}
<MID>    return [expr {$n * [calculate_factorial [expr {$n - 1}]]}]
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Tcl Tk

    return n * fn_factorial_toit(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_toit = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_toit = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

-- Transact-SQL Query Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

-- ==========================================
-- 1. Table Definitions and Queries
-- ==========================================

CREATE TABLE users (
    id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

SELECT username, COUNT(*) as activity_count
FROM users u
JOIN logs l ON u.id = l.user_id
WHERE l.created_at >= NOW() - INTERVAL '30 days'
GROUP BY username
HAVING COUNT(*) > 5
ORDER BY activity_count DESC;

-- ==========================================
-- 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
-- ==========================================
<PRE>
SELECT department_id, AVG(salary) as avg_sal
FROM employees
<SUF>
ORDER BY avg_sal DESC;
<MID>
WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'
GROUP BY department_id
<EOT>

# Turing Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

# ==========================================
# 1. Functions and Data Structures
# ==========================================

def greet(name: str) -> str:
    return f"Hello, {name} from Turing!"

def binary_search(arr: list, target: int) -> int:
    low, high = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        elif arr[mid] < target:
            low = mid + 1
        else:
            high = mid - 1
    return -1

# ==========================================
# 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
# ==========================================
<PRE>
def factorial(n: int) -> int:
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
<SUF>
<MID>
    return n * factorial(n - 1)
<EOT>

// TypeScript Production Features: Interfaces, Generics, Async/Await & Utility Types

export interface User {
  readonly id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'admin' | 'user' | 'guest';
  createdAt: Date;
}

export type ApiResponse<T> =
  | { success: true; data: T; timestamp: number }
  | { success: false; error: string; code: number };

export class HttpClient {
  private baseUrl: string;

  constructor(baseUrl: string) {
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, '');
  }

  async get<T>(endpoint: string, headers?: Record<string, string>): Promise<ApiResponse<T>> {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}${endpoint}`, {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...headers }
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        return { success: false, error: response.statusText, code: response.status };
      }
      const data: T = await response.json();
      return { success: true, data, timestamp: Date.now() };
    } catch (err) {
      return { success: false, error: (err as Error).message, code: 500 };
    }
  }
}

// Advanced TypeScript generic repository interface
export interface Repository<T extends { id: string }> {
  findById(id: string): Promise<T | null>;
  findAll(): Promise<T[]>;
  save(item: Omit<T, 'id'>): Promise<T>;
  delete(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
}

export class InMemoryRepository<T extends { id: string }> implements Repository<T> {
  private items = new Map<string, T>();

  async findById(id: string): Promise<T | null> {
    return this.items.get(id) ?? null;
  }

  async findAll(): Promise<T[]> {
    return Array.from(this.items.values());
  }

  async save(item: Omit<T, 'id'>): Promise<T> {
    const id = Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
    const newItem = { ...item, id } as T;
    this.items.set(id, newItem);
    return newItem;
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.items.delete(id);
  }
}

// ==========================================
// 2. Advanced Generics, Conditional Types & Utility Types
// ==========================================

type IsString<T> = T extends string ? true : false;

type DeepReadonly<T> = {
    readonly [P in keyof T]: T[P] extends object ? DeepReadonly<T[P]> : T[P];
};

interface UserProfile {
    id: number;
    details: {
        name: string;
        email: string;
    };
}

type ReadonlyUserProfile = DeepReadonly<UserProfile>;


// Modern TypeScript Generics, Discriminated Unions and Type Guards

export type AsyncState<T> =
  | { status: 'idle' }
  | { status: 'loading' }
  | { status: 'success'; data: T }
  | { status: 'error'; error: Error };

export function isSuccessState<T>(state: AsyncState<T>): state is { status: 'success'; data: T } {
  return state.status === 'success';
}

export class DataStore<T> {
  private state: AsyncState<T> = { status: 'idle' };

  public getState(): AsyncState<T> {
    return this.state;
  }

  public setSuccess(data: T): void {
    this.state = { status: 'success', data };
  }
}
// ==========================================
// Native TypeScript 5 Utility Types & Functions
// ==========================================

export type Nullable<T> = T | null | undefined;

export function filterNonNull<T>(arr: Array<Nullable<T>>): T[] {
  return arr.filter((item): item is T => item !== null && item !== undefined);
}

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Unreal Engine C++ & Unity C# Game Systems
// ==========================================

// ------------------------------------------
// Unreal Engine 5 C++ (ACharacter Derived)
// ------------------------------------------
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "GameFramework/Character.h"
#include "LLPTCharacter.generated.h"

DECLARE_DYNAMIC_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_TwoParams(FOnHealthChanged, float, CurrentHealth, float, MaxHealth);

UCLASS()
class LLPTNANO_API ALLPTCharacter : public ACharacter
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	ALLPTCharacter();

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;

public:
	virtual void Tick(float DeltaTime) override;
	virtual void SetupPlayerInputComponent(class UInputComponent* PlayerInputComponent) override;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadWrite, Category = "Attributes")
	float MaxHealth = 100.0f;

	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Attributes")
	float CurrentHealth;

	UPROPERTY(BlueprintAssignable, Category = "Events")
	FOnHealthChanged OnHealthChanged;

	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "Attributes")
	void ApplyDamage(float DamageAmount);
};

// Implementation
#include "LLPTCharacter.h"

ALLPTCharacter::ALLPTCharacter()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	CurrentHealth = MaxHealth;
}

void ALLPTCharacter::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	CurrentHealth = MaxHealth;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(CurrentHealth, MaxHealth);
}

void ALLPTCharacter::Tick(float DeltaTime)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaTime);
}

void ALLPTCharacter::SetupPlayerInputComponent(UInputComponent* PlayerInputComponent)
{
	Super::SetupPlayerInputComponent(PlayerInputComponent);
}

void ALLPTCharacter::ApplyDamage(float DamageAmount)
{
	if (DamageAmount <= 0.0f || CurrentHealth <= 0.0f) return;

	CurrentHealth = FMath::Max(0.0f, CurrentHealth - DamageAmount);
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(CurrentHealth, MaxHealth);

	if (CurrentHealth <= 0.0f) {
		// Handle Death
	}
}

// FIM Examples
// ==========================================

bool IsAlive() const {
<SUF>}
<MID>    return CurrentHealth > 0.0f;
<EOT>

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - V Language High Performance Systems
// ==========================================

module main

import json
import time

struct User {
pub mut:
	id        int
	name      string
	email     string
	is_active bool
	created_at time.Time
}

fn new_user(id int, name string, email string) !User {
	if name.len == 0 {
		return error('User name cannot be empty')
	}
	if !email.contains('@') {
		return error('Invalid email address')
	}
	return User{
		id: id
		name: name
		email: email
		is_active: true
		created_at: time.now()
	}
}

fn (u User) to_json() string {
	return json.encode(u)
}

fn process_users_concurrently(users []User) []string {
	mut results := []string{}
	mut threads := []thread string{}

	for u in users {
		threads << go fn (usr User) string {
			return 'Processed user: ${usr.name} <${usr.email}>'
		}(u)
	}

	for t in threads {
		results << t.wait()
	}

	return results
}

fn main() {
	u := new_user(1, 'V Lang Dev', 'vdev@example.com') or {
		println('Failed to create user: ${err}')
		return
	}
	println(u.to_json())
}

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>fn calculate_sum(numbers []int) int {
	mut total := 0
<SUF>	return total
}
<MID>	for num in numbers {
		total += num
	}
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: V Lang

# ======================================================================

' ==========================================
' LLPT Code Nano - VBA Excel Automation & VBScript
' ==========================================

Option Explicit

Public Sub ProcessCustomerExcelReport()
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets("SalesData")

    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, "A").End(xlUp).Row

    Dim i As Long
    Dim totalRevenue As Double
    totalRevenue = 0#

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False

    For i = 2 To lastRow
        Dim quantity As Long
        Dim unitPrice As Double

        quantity = ws.Cells(i, 3).Value
        unitPrice = ws.Cells(i, 4).Value

        Dim lineTotal As Double
        lineTotal = quantity * unitPrice
        ws.Cells(i, 5).Value = lineTotal

        If lineTotal >= 1000# Then
            ws.Cells(i, 5).Interior.Color = RGB(144, 238, 144) ' Light green
        End If

        totalRevenue = totalRevenue + lineTotal
    Next i

    ws.Cells(lastRow + 2, 4).Value = "Total Revenue:"
    ws.Cells(lastRow + 2, 5).Value = totalRevenue
    ws.Cells(lastRow + 2, 5).Font.Bold = True

    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    MsgBox "Excel Sales Report successfully processed! Total: " & FormatCurrency(totalRevenue), vbInformation
End Sub

    If price <= 0 Then
        CalculateDiscount = 0
<SUF>    End If
End Function
<MID>    Else
        CalculateDiscount = price * rate
<EOT>

; VERILOG Low-Level Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

; ==========================================
; 1. Registers and Instructions
; ==========================================

section .text
global _start

_start:
    mov eax, 1        ; system call for exit
    mov ebx, 0        ; return status 0
    int 0x80          ; execute syscall

; ==========================================
; 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
; ==========================================
<PRE>
add_numbers:
    mov eax, [esp+4]
<SUF>
    ret
<MID>
    add eax, [esp+8]
<EOT>

# 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Verse

fn_factorial_verse = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_verse(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_verse = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

# 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_verse = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

;; ==========================================
;; LLPT Code Nano - WebAssembly Text Format (WAT) Memory & Math
;; ==========================================

(module
  ;; Memory declaration (1 page = 64KB)
  (memory (export "memory") 1)

  ;; Exported function: Vector Dot Product (f32)
  (func (export "dot_product_f32")
        (param $a_ptr i32)
        (param $b_ptr i32)
        (param $len i32)
        (result f32)
    (local $i i32)
    (local $acc f32)

    (local.set $i (i32.const 0))
    (local.set $acc (f32.const 0.0))

    (block $break
      (loop $top
        (br_if $break (i32.ge_s (local.get $i) (local.get $len)))

        ;; acc += a[i] * b[i]
        (local.set $acc
          (f32.add
            (local.get $acc)
            (f32.mul
              (f32.load (i32.add (local.get $a_ptr) (i32.shl (local.get $i) (i32.const 2))))
              (f32.load (i32.add (local.get $b_ptr) (i32.shl (local.get $i) (i32.const 2))))
            )
          )
        )

        (local.set $i (i32.add (local.get $i) (i32.const 1)))
        (br $top)
      )
    )
    (local.get $acc)
  )
)

;; ==========================================
;; FIM Examples
;; ==========================================
<PRE>(func (export "multiply_i32") (param $x i32) (param $y i32) (result i32)
<SUF>)
<MID>  local.get $x
  local.get $y
  i32.mul
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Wasm Wat

; WATFIV Low-Level Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

; ==========================================
; 1. Registers and Instructions
; ==========================================

section .text
global _start

_start:
    mov eax, 1        ; system call for exit
    mov ebx, 0        ; return status 0
    int 0x80          ; execute syscall

; ==========================================
; 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
; ==========================================
<PRE>
add_numbers:
    mov eax, [esp+4]
<SUF>
    ret
<MID>
    add eax, [esp+8]

# Desenvolvimento Frontend, Backend, Full-Stack, APIs, Desktop, Mobile e Jogos

# ==============================================================================
# 1. Backend REST API com FastAPI, Validação Pydantic e Tratamento de Erros
# ==============================================================================

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, EmailStr
from typing import Dict

app = FastAPI(title="LLPT Code Nano API Service", version="1.0.0")

class UserCreate(BaseModel):
    username: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=50)
    email: EmailStr
    age: int = Field(..., ge=18, le=120)

class UserResponse(BaseModel):
    id: str
    username: str
    email: str
    is_active: bool = True

db_users: Dict[str, UserResponse] = {}

@app.post("/api/v1/users", response_model=UserResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def create_user(user_in: UserCreate):
    for u in db_users.values():
        if u.email == user_in.email:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail="Um usuário com este e-mail já existe."
            )
    import uuid
    user_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    user_obj = UserResponse(id=user_id, **user_in.model_dump())
    db_users[user_id] = user_obj
    return user_obj


# ==============================================================================
# 2. FastAPI Endpoints com Validação Pydantic
# ==============================================================================

    email: EmailStr
    password: str = Field(..., min_length=8)

### LINGUAGEM: Webassembly
### ID: webassembly | Seção 385/405
# ======================================================================

;; ==========================================
;; LLPT Code Nano - WebAssembly Text Format (WAT) & Low-Level Bytecode
;; ==========================================

(module
  ;; Memory declaration (1 page = 64KB)
  (memory (export "memory") 1)

  ;; Exported Function: Vector Add (i32 arrays)
  (func (export "vector_add") (param $ptr1 i32) (param $ptr2 i32) (param $out_ptr i32) (param $len i32)
    (local $i i32)
    (local.set $i (i32.const 0))
    (block $break
      (loop $top
        (br_if $break (i32.ge_s (local.get $i) (local.get $len)))

        ;; Load value from ptr1 + i*4
        (i32.store
          (i32.add (local.get $out_ptr) (i32.mul (local.get $i) (i32.const 4)))
          (i32.add
            (i32.load (i32.add (local.get $ptr1) (i32.mul (local.get $i) (i32.const 4))))
            (i32.load (i32.add (local.get $ptr2) (i32.mul (local.get $i) (i32.const 4))))
          )
        )

        (local.set $i (i32.add (local.get $i) (i32.const 1)))
        (br $top)
      )
    )
  )

  ;; Exported Function: Factorial (Iterative)
  (func (export "factorial") (param $n i64) (result i64)
    (local $result i64)
    (local.set $result (i64.const 1))
    (block $done
      (loop $loop
        (br_if $done (i64.le_s (local.get $n) (i64.const 1)))
        (local.set $result (i64.mul (local.get $result) (local.get $n)))
        (local.set $n (i64.sub (local.get $n) (i64.const 1)))
        (br $loop)
      )
    )
    (local.get $result)
  )
)

;; ==========================================
;; FIM Examples
;; ==========================================
<PRE>(func (export "add_two") (param $a i32) (param $b i32) (result i32)
<SUF>)
<MID>  local.get $a
  local.get $b
  i32.add
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Webassembly

# ======================================================================

(* ==========================================
   LLPT Code Nano - Wolfram Language & Mathematica Symbolic Computation
   ========================================== *)

(* Functional Pipeline & Matrix Manipulations *)
ProcessDataset[data_List, minThreshold_Numeric] := Module[
    {cleanedData, aggregated, model},

    cleanedData = Select[data, #["Value"] >= minThreshold &];

    aggregated = GroupBy[cleanedData, #["Category"] &, Mean[#[[All, "Value"]]] &];

    model = LinearModelFit[
        Table[{i, cleanedData[[i, "Value"]]}, {i, Length[cleanedData]}],
        x, x
    ];

    <|
        "CleanedCount" -> Length[cleanedData],
        "AggregatedMeans" -> aggregated,
        "RSquared" -> model["RSquared"],
        "Formula" -> model["BestFit"]
    |>
]

(* Differential Equation Solver *)
SolveHarmonicOscillator[k_, m_, x0_, v0_, tMax_] := Module[
    {sol},
    sol = NDSolveValue[
        {m x''[t] + k x[t] == 0, x[0] == x0, x'[0] == v0},
        x, {t, 0, tMax}
    ];
    Plot[sol[t], {t, 0, tMax}, PlotLabel -> "Harmonic Oscillator Trajectory"]
]

(* ==========================================
   FIM Examples
   ========================================== *)
<PRE>SymbolicFactorial[n_Integer] := Module[{},
    If[n <= 1, 1,
<SUF>    ]
]
<MID>    n * SymbolicFactorial[n - 1]
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Wolfram Mathematica

<!-- ==========================================
     LLPT Code Nano - XSLT 3.0 & XPath/XQuery Transformations
     ========================================== -->

<xsl:stylesheet version="3.0"
                xmlns:xsl="http://www.w3.org/1999/XSL/Transform"
                xmlns:xs="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema"
                xmlns:llpt="http://llpt.dev/functions"
                exclude-result-prefixes="xs llpt">

    <xsl:output method="html" version="5.0" encoding="UTF-8" indent="yes"/>

    <!-- Custom XSLT Function -->
    <xsl:function name="llpt:calculate-tier" as="xs:string">
        <xsl:param name="revenue" as="xs:decimal"/>
        <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$revenue >= 100000.0">Gold Tier</xsl:when>
            <xsl:when test="$revenue >= 25000.0">Silver Tier</xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>Standard Tier</xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:function>

    <!-- Template Matching Root -->
    <xsl:template match="/customers">
        <html>
            <head>
                <title>LLPT Customer Audit Report</title>
                <style>
                    table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }
                    th, td { border: 1px solid #ccc; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }
                    th { background-color: #f2f2f2; }
                </style>
            </head>
            <body>
                <h1>Customer Revenue Summary</h1>
                <table>
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>ID</th>
                            <th>Name</th>
                            <th>Revenue</th>
                            <th>Tier</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        <xsl:apply-templates select="customer[status = 'Active']">
                            <xsl:sort select="revenue" data-type="number" order="descending"/>
                        </xsl:apply-templates>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </body>
        </html>
    </xsl:template>

    <xsl:template match="customer">
        <tr>
            <td><xsl:value-of select="@id"/></td>
            <td><xsl:value-of select="name"/></td>
            <td>$<xsl:value-of select="format-number(revenue, '#,##0.00')"/></td>
            <td><xsl:value-of select="llpt:calculate-tier(xs:decimal(revenue))"/></td>
        </tr>
    </xsl:template>

</xsl:stylesheet>

     FIM Examples
     ========================================== -->

    <div class="product-item">
<SUF>    </div>
</xsl:template>
<MID>        <h3><xsl:value-of select="title"/></h3>

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Xtext Real Idiomatic Code
// ==========================================

// 1. Functional and Algorithmic Implementations in Xtext

fn_factorial_xtext = function(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    return n * fn_factorial_xtext(n - 1);
}

fn_process_array_xtext = function(arr) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        sum += arr[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2. Data Filtering and Business Logic

fn_filter_positive_xtext = function(items) {
    let result = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
        if (items[i] > 0) {
            result.push(items[i]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

### LINGUAGEM: Zig
### ID: zig | Seção 403/405
# ======================================================================

// ==========================================
// LLPT Code Nano - Zig 0.11+ High Performance Memory Management & Comptime
// ==========================================

const std = @import("std");
const Allocator = std.mem.Allocator;
const testing = std.testing;

pub const UserStatus = enum {
    active,
    pending,
    suspended,
};

pub const User = struct {
    id: u64,
    name: []const u8,
    email: []const u8,
    status: UserStatus,

    pub fn format(
        self: User,
        comptime fmt: []const u8,
        options: std.fmt.FormatOptions,
        writer: anytype,
    ) !void {
        _ = fmt;
        _ = options;
        try writer.print("User[id={d}, name={s}, email={s}, status={s}]", .{
            self.id,
            self.name,
            self.email,
            @tagName(self.status),
        });
    }
};

pub const UserRegistry = struct {
    allocator: Allocator,
    users: std.ArrayList(User),

    pub fn init(allocator: Allocator) UserRegistry {
        return UserRegistry{
            .allocator = allocator,
            .users = std.ArrayList(User).init(allocator),
        };
    }

    pub fn deinit(self: *UserRegistry) void {
        for (self.users.items) |user| {
            self.allocator.free(user.name);
            self.allocator.free(user.email);
        }
        self.users.deinit();
    }

    pub fn register(self: *UserRegistry, id: u64, name: []const u8, email: []const u8) !void {
        const dup_name = try self.allocator.dupe(u8, name);
        errdefer self.allocator.free(dup_name);

        const dup_email = try self.allocator.dupe(u8, email);
        errdefer self.allocator.free(dup_email);

        const user = User{
            .id = id,
            .name = dup_name,
            .email = dup_email,
            .status = .active,
        };

        try self.users.append(user);
    }
};

// Comptime Generic Function
pub fn genericVectorSum(comptime T: type, slice: []const T) T {
    var sum: T = 0;
    for (slice) |item| {
        sum += item;
    }
    return sum;
}

test "UserRegistry registration test" {
    var gpa = std.heap.GeneralPurposeAllocator(.{}){};
    defer _ = gpa.deinit();
    const allocator = gpa.allocator();

    var registry = UserRegistry.init(allocator);
    defer registry.deinit();

    try registry.register(1, "Zig Engine", "zig@example.com");
    try testing.expectEqual(@as(usize, 1), registry.users.items.len);
    try testing.expectEqualStrings("Zig Engine", registry.users.items[0].name);
}

// ==========================================
// FIM Examples
// ==========================================
<PRE>pub fn fibonacci(n: u32) u32 {
    if (n == 0) return 0;
    if (n == 1) return 1;
<SUF>    return a + b;
}
<MID>    var a: u32 = 0;
    var b: u32 = 1;
    var i: u32 = 2;
    while (i <= n) : (i += 1) {
        const temp = a + b;
        a = b;
        b = temp;
    }
<EOT>

### FIM DA LINGUAGEM: Zig

# Zsh Scripting Dataset - Idiomatic Code Examples

# ==========================================
# 1. Basic Commands and Logic
# ==========================================

# Example 1: Hello World & Variables in Zsh
echo "Running Zsh script"
VAL=42
if [ "$VAL" -gt 0 ]; then
    echo "Value is positive: $VAL"
fi

# Example 2: Functions and Loops in Zsh
process_items() {
    local items=("$@")
    for item in "${items[@]}"; do
        echo "Processing item: $item"
    done
}

# ==========================================
# 2. Fill-In-The-Middle (FIM) Example
# ==========================================
<PRE>
#!/usr/bin/env zsh
calculate_sum() {
    local total=0
<SUF>
    echo $total
}
<MID>
    for num in "$@"; do
        total=$((total + num))
    done
<EOT>
